# Карта на клиента: ревизия 3 (the staff client card, revision 3)

> **Phase 4b (2026-10) — read `CLAUDE.md` Laws 1–9 for the current shape.** `libs/domain/*` and `libs/application/*` were deleted in Phase 4b (2026-10); links and paths into them below are dead history.

Status: design spec, **revision 3, second issue**, 2026-09-29. The lead designer's answer to the owner's round-2 feedback **F1–F11** on the BUILT client card, written against the lead's brief **L1–L11** and the binding rulings **R2-A … R2-M**, and revised after two adversarial reviews (taste and owner lens; feasibility, house rules and build plan). Every finding of both reviews is answered in **§9**. It is a NEW file (R2-M): the first spec, `docs/design-research/client-card-redesign.md` (below: **the first spec**), stays as the record of what was built and is superseded only where §0.2 says so.

**What the owner rejected** was the taste, not the engineering: grey rectangles, a brown-red ink, a head packed against the leading edge, grey-on-grey cards, rails that hide medals, a bar under a medal, a records strip at the foot of a page. **What this revision builds** is one sentence long: _spacious, centred on the person, colour carried by surfaces and glyphs in the one orange, white elevated cards, discs, grids and rings._ If a region of a wireframe below could be mistaken for the rejected build, it is a defect of this spec.

Three rules were added by the reviews and hold on every page:

1. **One orange slab per page.** At most ONE object taller than 52 is solid orange: the birthday band on the glance, the pass on the wallet. Everything else that is orange is a mark (a disc, a ring, a glyph, a display figure).
2. **One fact, once, in one encoding too.** A count is printed once per screen, and a ring, a row of stamps and a sentence never say the same number together.
3. **A role has one size.** The type ladder of §2.0 gives every role one rung; nothing but the name is set at the name's size.

Conventions: Bulgarian copy first, an English gloss after it. **NEW** marks anything that does not exist in the repo today; **CHANGED** marks an existing piece whose contract or look changes; everything else is cited by its real path and was checked against the working tree on 2026-09-29 (grep). Seed figures are computed from the book (LR7), so the wireframes' numbers are illustrations, never fixtures.

Evidence (scratchpad, 2026-09-29): `v2/owner-feedback.md`, `v2/brief-v2.md`, `v2/lead-rulings-v2.md`, the eight reports in `v2/research/` (`accent-audit`, `ds-defects`, `visits-reuse`, `rhythm-admin`, `profile-head`, `wallet`, `fresha-coverage`, `achievements-game`), the two reviews `v2/critique-taste.md` and `v2/critique-feasibility.md`, the owner's liked screens `v2/account/*.png`, the criticised captures `wave-d/r7/shots/*.png` and the prototypes `v2/ds-defects/{hero,discs}/*.png`. Mobbin links are cited where a pattern is taken.

---

### 0.3 What is kept

- **The page stack** (first spec §2): `push` / `replace` / `pop`, `returnTo`, `returnScrollTop`, the depth cap 4, ✓ = `popToLadder`, ‹ walks one step, the focus and scroll rules of §2.3. Two page kinds join (§5).
- **The store and the presenter approach**: `ClientCardStore` (`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-card.store.ts`) holds data, pure presenters word it, each module is a `Slice<T>`, an absent key is an absent module. The new pages get NEW presenters and NEW store members beside the built ones (§4.9); the built ones are deleted in the last wave, never rewritten in place (§7.0).
- **The data layer**: ports `PROFILE_PORT`, `AVATAR_UPLOADER`, `CLIENT_STATS_READER`, `WAITLIST_READER`, `CLIENT_HISTORY_READER`, `CLIENT_PHOTOS_READER`, `LOYALTY_READER`, `LOYALTY_CATALOG_READER`; the loyalty read model in `libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/loyalty/`; the lazy adapters. Four ports join (§4).
- **The behaviours**: the reward ticket's routing (owner only, «Използвай» applies then pushes `discount`, the ticket stands stamped on return), «Повтори», the waitlist line's rule, the D-9 gate (nothing under the head paints before `users/{uid}` answers), absence over disabling, R59 dates, no countdowns.
- **The photo page** (§3.7) and the shared states (§3.8) of the first spec.
- **Test ids where the element survives** (§0.4).

### 0.5 Deviations from the brief and the rulings, and what is escalated

The brief lets a report's finding stand against a decision only where the decision "breaks accessibility or a house rule", and asks for this table. Rows marked **taste** break no rule: they follow a review's finding that the ruling's literal form reads worse than its intent. Each keeps the ruling's form ONE input or ONE value away, so the lead can overrule it without a redesign.

| #    | Ruling                                                                         | What this spec does                                                                                                                                                                  | Why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Kind                       | To restore the ruling's form                                                         |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DV-A | R2-G, L10: the chips are "pinned, wrapping" and lead the page                  | The chip band sits **in flow, under «Рекорди»**, wrapping, never sticky                                                                                                              | Seven chips measure ≈ 614 pt: two rows at 390 (141 pt of permanent chrome with the bar, 17 % of the screen), three at 330 (185 pt, 22 %). A band that size covers the control the focus returns to (WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11); pinning it from a feature copies a DS recipe (reuse, never copy) and needs changes in `modal-sheet.css` that the round does not otherwise make; and a filter above the records contradicts F11 | accessibility + house rule | A DS pinned band (one row: the chosen chip and a «Категории» menu) is listed in §8.2 |
| DV-B | R2-H, L11: «Как се печели» as numbered steps AND «Какво се брои» with pictures | ONE block «Как се печели»: the family's requirement sentence, the pictures of what counts, who and where, the exclusion once. No numbered steps                                      | Every metric of the catalogue is a COUNT (`METRICS`, `badge-vocabulary.ts:108-133`), so the steps were derived, identical on every medal, and repeated the progress figure and the exclusion: the house rule "one fact, once per screen"                                                                                                                                                                                | house rule                 | `DetailHowVm.steps` is reserved; a family with distinct actions would fill it        |

| ES-3 | R2-A D4                                                      | The week strip is the screen he opens first, and it keeps the brown                                                                                                                                                                   | Deferred as ruled, asked FIRST                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | the owner, at review 1 (Q1) |

## 1. The accent law (R2-A)

> **One orange. Colour is a shape; words are ink.**
>
> 1. Everything you SEE as colour — a fill, a wash, a ring, a track, a dot, a disc, a glyph — is `--sys-color-accent` (#f26b22), the same value in both themes. There is no darker or lighter cut of it.
> 2. Words are ink: primary, or secondary where they are quiet. A small word is never tinted. When a small word has to feel orange it gets an orange THING: a glyph or a dot before it, a wash capsule behind it (the word in primary ink), an accent underline under a link.
> 3. A **display figure** may itself be `--sys-color-accent`: ≥ 24 px, or ≥ 18.66 px at weight ≥ 700 (axe's large-text test). In the type ladder (`libs/ui/tokens/tokens.css:31-42`) that is `title2`, `title`, `largeTitle`, `extraLargeTitle` as they are, and `title3` only at weight 700. On a WHITE or elevated surface in light (3.04:1); on any opaque surface in dark (≥ 4.97:1). Never on a grey segment, the muted card, a fill or a wash (2.84 → 2.40 in light).
> 4. On the SOLID accent: white marks and white display figures only (3.04:1). No small words. A small fact that belongs to an orange object stands in ink on the object's white part (the pass's foot, the ticket's body).
> 5. **One slab.** At most ONE object taller than 52 per page is solid orange. A mark (a disc, an emblem, a medal's face, a stamp: 52 or smaller) may be solid as often as the page needs.

Why it is the only honest rule (accent-audit §5): the AA line is a luminance line. Every hue that passes 4.5:1 on white has OKLCH L ≈ 0.57–0.59 against the accent's 0.684, and at that lightness an orange hue is rust. There is no second orange; there is orange and ink.

### 1.5 The guard

`libs/ui/tokens/src/lib/accent-label.spec.ts` is **narrowed** to the three deferred readers (it keeps the `--sys-color-secondary-label-on-fill` block at `:593-652`, which is unrelated). A NEW `libs/ui/tokens/src/lib/accent-rule.spec.ts` guards the law. It reads stylesheets with their COMMENTS STRIPPED and skips the two theme files' own declarations, so a token's definition and a comment that names it never trip it.

1. No stylesheet under `libs/ui`, `libs/features`, `apps/web/src`, `apps/showcase/src` READS `--sys-color-promo-label` (from the end of W1 it is not defined either); `--sys-color-accent-label` is read by `date-badge.css` and `staff-dashboard.chrome.css` and by nothing else.
2. Both themes: `--sys-color-accent` ≥ 3:1 on page, surface and elevated card; `< 3` on the light muted card and on the list group's segment (`--sys-color-surface-secondary`), asserted so the exclusion is a recorded fact.
3. Both themes: `--sys-color-foreground` ≥ 4.6:1 on every accent wash read from its rule (`button.css` rest / hover / selected, `date-tile.css`, `--sys-alpha-tint`, `--sys-alpha-segment-selected`) over page, surface, segment and elevated card.
4. `--sys-color-on-accent` on the accent ≥ 3 and < 4.5 (marks and display figures only).
5. **Accent as `color`.** Every rule that sets `color: var(--sys-color-accent)` or `var(--sys-color-promo)` either (a) ends in a GLYPH selector (`.ui-icon`, `*__glyph`, `*__check`, `*__mark`, `[data-icon-only]`), or (b) sets a display font in the same rule (≥ 24 px, or ≥ 18.66 px at weight ≥ 700), or (c) is in the spec's recorded table `ACCENT_INK_ALLOWED` with its reason. The table opens with the eight rules the tree holds today, all glyphs by their own part names: `button.css:434`, `staff-day-pill.css:62`, `staff-sheet-pill.css:114`, `client-appointments.css:126`, `booking-waitlisted-step.css:31`, `booking-select.css:71`, `apps/showcase/src/styles.css:123`, `async-image.page.css:17`. A rule that is none of the three fails.
6. **Templates.** `uiForegroundStyle="accent"` or `"promo"`, written as an attribute OR as a literal inside a bound expression (`[uiForegroundStyle]="… ? 'promo' : …"`), appears only on `ui-icon`, or on an element whose `uiFont` is `title2`, `title`, `largeTitle` or `extraLargeTitle` (or `title3` with `uiWeight="bold"`).
7. **White on the accent.** Every rule that sets `color: var(--sys-color-on-accent)` is in the spec's recorded table `ON_ACCENT_ALLOWED`, each row with its reason. The table opens with the fifteen rules the tree holds today:

| Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Reason                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `medal.css:175-178` (the earned disc), `gauge.css:129-133` (a filled stamp), `gauge.css:321-324` (the check), `count-stepper.css:55-58`, `map.css:69-73`, `map.css:155-157`, `showcase-gallery.component.css:94-96`, `service-card.component.css:53-57`, `client-appointments.css:279-283` | a glyph or a check: a mark                                                         |
| `date-badge.css:100-104`, `staff-dashboard.chrome.css:541-543`                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | a bold 20 px number: a display figure                                              |
| `pass-card.css` (the figure, the unit), `occasion-band.css` (the title)                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | display figures, NEW                                                               |
| `staff-time-grid.css:311-314`, `staff-time-grid.state.css:504-513`, `staff-timeline.css:116-126`                                                                                                                                                                                           | **deferred** (§1.4, Q1): small figures, recorded as the law's three known breaches |

`chip.css:59-62` leaves the list with A1's change. A NEW rule of this kind fails until it is added WITH a reason. 8. **One slab** (rule 5) is guarded where a page exists, not in a stylesheet: each page body's spec asserts its own count (the glance: no `.ui-pass-card`, at most one `.ui-occasion-band`; the wallet: exactly one `.ui-pass-card`, no `.ui-occasion-band`), and the capture script of §7.6 counts, on every page of the stack, the elements taller than 52 whose computed background is the accent: at most one.

`libs/ui/tokens/project.json:11-31` lists the new spec's inputs (`badge.css`, `chip.css`, `modifiers.css`, `coupon-card.css`, `occasion-band.css`, `pass-card.css`, `quick-actions.css`, `apps/web/src/styles.css`, the three deferred schedule stylesheets).

## 2. Pages

### 2.0 Frame and conventions (every page of the client stack)

- **Frame.** 390 × 844 pt phone, the visit sheet at full height. Sheet top 34, bar 61 (chips ‹ ✕ at y 42–86, the bar's bottom edge at y 95), content starts 16 under the bar (y 111). Page inset `--ui-sheet-inset` = 17.6, so the **column is 354.8 pt** (355 below). The dock's top edge is ≈ 776. At 330 the column is 295; in the ≥ 761 dialog (576 wide, inset 24) it is 528.
- **Space.** Module → module is `--sys-space-loose` (32), the body's own host gap (CHANGED from `comfortable` 24). Heading → body is `compact` (8) under a heading row that is always the `small` rung tall (36, §3.2). Card → card inside one module is `regular` (16). Head → actions is 32 (R2-C). Name → its quiet line is `tight` (4): the line belongs to the name.
- **Surfaces** (R2-E). An object of value is a `ui-card uiTone="elevated"` (`libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/card/card.ts`): white with `--sys-elevation-raised` in light, `--sys-color-surface-elevated` in dark. Padding `comfortable` (24) on a feature card, `regular` (16) inside a 2-up or 3-up grid. A chronological list and the exits are `ul[uiListGroup]` rows (the `/account/appointments` look the owner likes). `uiTone="muted"` does not appear in the stack.
- **One slab** (§1 rule 5). ONE object per page may be a solid-orange surface: the birthday band on the glance, the pass on the wallet. No other page has one.
- **Headings** are `ui-section-header uiHeaderProminence="increased"` WITHOUT `uiIcon`: the cards carry the glyphs, on discs. `title3` 20/600, primary (CHANGED from `headline`, §3.2). One trailing action at most, `small`, inside the column.
- **Type: a role has one rung.**

| Role                                 | Rung                              | Ink                                                                                           | Where                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| a pushed page's title                | `largeTitle` 36/800               | primary                                                                                       | «Постижения», «Рекорди», «Посещения», «Движение», the scope pages                                      |
| the NAME under a 112 face            | `title` 28/700                    | primary                                                                                       | the glance, the medal detail. **Nothing else on those two pages is set at 28**                         |
| the page's ONE hero figure           | `largeTitle` 36/800               | white on the pass; primary elsewhere                                                          | the pass's balance, the detail's «23», the records page's featured figure                              |
| a section heading                    | `title3` 20/600                   | primary                                                                                       | every module                                                                                           |
| a card's figure                      | `title2` 24/700, tabular          | accent on white where it is a value you hold (points, a ticket, a voucher); primary elsewhere | the glance's points, a ticket's value, a record card, the rhythm's count                               |
| an OBJECT's headline, a row's figure | `headline` 18/600                 | primary                                                                                       | the visit card's services, a ticket without a value, a record row's figure, every ring's centre figure |
| a row's title                        | `body` 15/500                     | primary                                                                                       | list rows                                                                                              |
| a tile's or a status card's title    | `callout` 14/600                  | primary                                                                                       | record cards, reward tiles, the stamp card, the status card                                            |
| a quiet line                         | `callout` 14, or `subheadline` 13 | secondary; its FACTS primary/500                                                              | under a name, under a headline                                                                         |
| a caption                            | `footnote` 12, `caption` 11       | secondary                                                                                     | dates, units, node labels                                                                              |

No two adjacent texts share both size and tone (L5): a 20/600 heading never sits over an 18/600 line without a card's padding between them, and an 18/600 headline is always followed by a 14 line. Tabular figures everywhere.

- **Ink** (§1). Words are primary or secondary. `--sys-color-accent` is for glyphs, discs, washes, rings and display figures on white. A fact inside a quiet line is `span uiForegroundStyle="primary" uiWeight="medium"` (§1.3).
- **Motion** (R2-L). Nothing new animates for staff: the house press and hover, the existing count-up rules. Rings and gauges draw settled.
- **Absence.** Data-less or role-less = absent, never disabled. A module whose slice fails keeps its heading and shows a compact `ui-notice` with «Опитай пак» (`visits.retry`).
- **One fact, once** per screen, **in one encoding**: a count is a figure OR a ring's centre OR a row of stamps, never two of them in one card.
- **Wireframe legend.** `╭╮╰╯` a white elevated card; `┏┓┗┛` a solid-orange surface; `░` an accent wash; `◖ ◗` a ticket's notches, `╌` its tear; `( )` a disc or a ring; `(( ))` the promoted disc (accent wash); `(■)` a solid-orange mark; `[ ]` a capsule (`span[uiBadge]`); `⟦ ⟧` the prominent pill; `◔ ◑ ◕ ●` a medal's ring, a quarter to full; `◌` a dashed silhouette; `›` pushes; `▒` `ui-skeleton`; `‹role›` role-gated; `**x**` a fact in primary ink inside a secondary line.

---

Above the fold: who (a face you cannot miss, the status on it), how to reach them, the reward to use now, what we did last time, who cut and what he said. **Budget**: with every module present the content runs 1 700 pt (two screens and a bit); the usual client (no ticket, no next visit) runs ≈ 1 390 pt. Seven objects, where the first issue of this spec drew eleven: the wallet and the achievements page are not drawn again on the glance, they are SUMMARISED (one card each), and the rhythm, which the first issue printed three times here, is one fact of one line.

What changed against the first issue, by finding: the points are a white card, not a second orange slab (rule 5); the face has 35 of air, not 11; the quiet line hugs the name; the two visit cards read in the same order; the voucher, the stamps and the rhythm are facts of one line; the three medals are named; the records are a line, not two more cards.

#### 2.1.2 The birthday card: three states and one quiet variant

**(a) Birthday today, the client holds the gift** (Мартин Илиев, the appointment's owner). The card sits directly under the head, BEFORE the actions (L3); E does not repeat the grant. It is the page's one orange slab.

```

       │ ┃     ▮          ▪            ✦            ▮            ▪          ┃ │    white disc 44, cake 24 accent · title2 24/700 white

**(b) Birthday today, no gift to use** (no grant, a party member, a gone visit, offline): the band alone, fully rounded, 96 tall, no perforation. It is a statement, not a control.

**(c) Not a birthday**: BD is absent. A birthday grant still held on a later day (it is valid 30 days) is an ordinary white ticket in E, titled «Рожден ден», «−20 %»: the solid band is reserved for the day itself. The wallet draws it the same way on EVERY day, the birthday included (the wallet's slab is the pass).

**(d) Designer's decision (L3 left it open): the birthday within the next 7 days** is one quiet line in BD's place, `ui-notice uiTone="accent" uiIcon="client.birthday"`: «Рожден ден · петък, 2.10» and, when the year is known, « · 34 г.». A date, never a countdown. No band, no flecks.

**Without a year of birth** the band's title is «Рожден ден днес».

**Applied** (the gift is on this visit's draft bill): the band stays; the body is the ticket's applied grammar (`article[uiCouponCard] uiApplied`, the check on the figure's corner, a real «Премахни» where the pill stood).

References: a two-part celebratory card whose festive read comes from marks at full contrast over the whole band: [Zomato](https://mobbin.com/screens/51f6c5fd-f331-4575-a563-ca70ece58ebd), [Grill'd](https://mobbin.com/screens/f1a0a335-435b-42c3-89cb-b9a431c4d86d).

#### 2.1.4 Behaviours (deltas over the first spec §3.1.3)

- **The loyalty card pushes the wallet** at its top; «Всички ›» does the same. The three facts of its holdings line are not controls (one card, one target).
- **The records line pushes «Рекорди»** (`clientRecords`, depth 2); «38 ›» pushes the collection.
- **The birthday gift is the reward ticket's routing, unchanged**: «Използвай» applies the grant through the discount page's own path and pushes `discount`; on ‹ the card stands with its body stamped and takes the focus.
- **The photo grid shows three** on a phone; there is no photos page in this round.
- Everything else (the medals push their detail, «Повтори», the waitlist rule, the destructive row, nothing writes outside the draft) is as built.

#### 2.1.5 States

| State                          | What the page shows                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading                        | The head renders at 0 ms from the seat's snapshot (the avatar's initials, the name). The ring box is empty, the plate absent. Everything below waits for `users/{uid}` (the D-9 gate). Placeholders, each `aria-busy`: BD none (a birthday is never promised by a skeleton); D at once from the snapshot's phone; E a ticket-shaped `ui-skeleton`; H one 117 card; F one 128 card; G one 214 card; I three 113 × 141 tiles. Shimmer ≤ 3 × 1.4 s, static under reduced motion |
| Empty (a new client, 0 visits) | A5 «Първо посещение»; «Повтори» absent (three discs, centred at the pitch); H1 absent, H0's count «0» still pushes; with an account F shows the welcome points; **G absent** until the first earned level; I absent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Error + retry                  | One module: its heading and a compact `ui-notice` with «Опитай пак» (`client-retry-{region}`). Two or more: one notice under the head «Част от данните не се заредиха.» and the failed modules keep their headings only. The profile itself: the head, one notice «Данните за клиента не се заредиха.», D and K                                                                                                                                                              |
| Offline                        | C″ under the head; the discs stay (OS hand-offs); «Повтори», E, BD's «Използвай» (the card falls to state b) and K are absent; modules show cached values without skeletons                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Flags off                      | `loyalty.tiers` off: no ring, no plate. `loyalty.points` off: F1 leads with its first holding as the figure («25,00 €», or «3 от 10»), or F is absent. `loyalty.achievements` off: no stamps fact, no G. `loyalty.streaks` off OR the rule in force is off: no rhythm fact. `client.profile` off: the editor's stub, as today                                                                                                                                                |
| Paused client                  | The avatar wears the WARNING ring (it replaces the tier ring); A5 is the pause sentence; E and BD's gift absent (the band stays on a birthday); the rhythm fact absent; H3 absent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Guest / walk-in                | The avatar's initials, no ring, no plate; A5 «Гост · без профил»; D only when the seat holds a phone; K. Nothing else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Party member                   | Every read keys on the member's own uid; E absent and BD falls to state (b): a companion's rewards cannot be spent on this visit; the quote shows only on visits the member booked                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Long name                      | Wraps, centred and balanced, to two lines (a third if it must); the bar's compact title truncates. «Александър Константинов-Христов» at 330 takes three lines of 28 and the actions move down 60                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| No avatar                      | The initials on the avatar's own fill; the ring and the plate are unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

#### 2.1.6 330 pt and the ≥ 761 dialog

- **330.** Column 295. The ring box clears the chips by 39 each side. Four discs at a 73 pitch («Обади се» is ≈ 52 wide at 12 pt). A5 wraps to two balanced lines. The band keeps a REDUCED set of eight marks under 360 and its title stays one line («Рожден ден · 34 г.» is ≈ 190 wide at 24/700 beside the 56 of the disc: 246 of 263). H1's thumbs stay 72. F1's holdings line wraps to two. G1 keeps three medals (tile 82). The photo grid stays three columns (93 × 116).
- **≥ 761.** Column 528. The head is unchanged (the hero does not grow). D keeps the 88 pitch, centred. BD and E keep the column. H1 sits beside H2 when both exist (2-up). F1 and G1 sit side by side (2-up); G1 shows three medals. The photo grid is five columns, two rows.

Above the fold: the pass, the four discs, the voucher and the reward. "What can he use today" is answered without a scroll. References: the hero and the discs are [Revolut's home](https://mobbin.com/screens/5be4e584-20cf-45a5-aade-d505997121b7); a pass prints its values as plain type, with no capsule on it ([World of Hyatt](https://mobbin.com/screens/ff4374f1-7ddd-4e41-87b3-33e9a6bdab4a), [Shangri-La Circle](https://mobbin.com/screens/1c46893c-c975-4b9a-854d-3839e3dc7d7f)) and the holder on a plain part ([HBX](https://mobbin.com/screens/42b7b300-afd1-4159-9e09-cefc486c111d)); the stack of passes is [Setel](https://mobbin.com/screens/25f69654-dc9a-4d11-b5b5-ee57def75b64); the stamp card is [Honest Greens](https://mobbin.com/screens/6dc61bf0-a764-450e-857c-502d99dd769c); the month-grouped statement is [Setel's history](https://mobbin.com/screens/f38c3947-3f24-43b8-912f-4009256c4bd8) with [Amazon's running balance](https://mobbin.com/screens/cce37c8d-9317-4398-9f09-331ecfd66201).

**One fact, one encoding, on this page.** The stamps are counted by the stamps (no ring, no «3 от 10», no «още 7»). The rhythm's health is the figure and the track (a capsule appears only when the phase is NOT healthy: «До 9.11», «Гратис до 16.11», «Защитен», «Прекъснат»). The status is a ring that holds what remains, one line, and the ladder (no capsule). «Злато» is on the pass and under the ladder's node, nowhere else. «Използвай» is the disc, nowhere else. «Безплатно подстригване» names a ticket HELD and a reward in the grid (two states of one thing); the stamp card is «Карта с печати», as on the glance.

#### 2.2.2 Region spec

Body: `lib-client-loyalty` (`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-loyalty/`), re-drawn. The page's `h2` is `uiVisuallyHidden` (the photo page's precedent, `staff-visit-editor.html:931`), so the container stays labelled and the bar names the page from rest (`pageHasNoLargeTitle`, §5). The host carries `data-sheet-fade="tight"` (NEW hook, §3.3): the bar's fade keeps to the bar, so no white haze lies over the orange pass the moment the page moves.

There is no link from this page to the admin's rule page in this round: a `routerLink` inside the sheet would leave through the focus trap and drop the visit's draft without asking (the app has no `canDeactivate`, and the editor's discard guard has no destination). The admin reaches the rule from the shell's menu (§3.17); the link is listed in §8.2.

#### 2.2.3 The four discs (R2-F; ES-2)

A disc that cannot be used is ABSENT (the run closes up, centred at the 88 pitch). Only discs that work today are drawn (fresha-coverage §5.3).

| Disc                  | Face                                                                                                                                                                                       | Does                                                                                                                                                                                 | Present when                                                                            |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| «Използвай» / "Use"   | promoted (`uiProminence="accent"`), glyph `promo.coupon`; with MORE THAN ONE ready ticket a primary-fill count badge on the disc's corner (`span[uiBadge uiProminence="increased"]`, §1.3) | with exactly one ready ticket: `openReward(grantId)` (it arrives applied); with several: pushes `discount` on the owner's coupons                                                    | the subject is the appointment's owner, the visit is not gone, online, a reward is held |
| «Код» / "Code"        | neutral, glyph `loyalty.enterCode` (NEW key)                                                                                                                                               | pushes `discount`; the focus arrives on the page's container as on every page (no control is ever auto-focused), the code field is its first control and the scanner one tap from it | the owner's card, the visit not gone, online                                            |
| «Награди» / "Rewards" | neutral, glyph `loyalty.reward`                                                                                                                                                            | scrolls to WF under the bar and focuses its heading (`tabindex="-1"`)                                                                                                                | `loyalty.points` on and the catalogue holds a reward                                    |
| «История» / "History" | neutral, glyph `loyalty.ledger`                                                                                                                                                            | pushes `clientLedger`                                                                                                                                                                | `loyalty.points` on                                                                     |

#### 2.2.4 States

| State         | What the page shows                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading       | The pass as ONE 224 `ui-skeleton` of its own radius; four 52 discs; two 120 tickets; each later section its heading over one block. `aria-busy` per section                                                                                                                                                     |
| Empty         | No account: the page is never reached (the glance has no F). An account with nothing held: WC0. No ledger yet: WG absent                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Error + retry | The account's read failed: the bar, one `ui-notice` «Лоялността не се зареди.» with «Опитай пак» (`rewards-retry`). A section's own read (vouchers, catalogue, activity): its heading over a compact notice (`rewards-retry-{section}`)                                                                         |
| Offline       | `rewards-offline` notice under the bar; «Използвай» and «Код» absent; cached values, no skeletons                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Flags off     | `loyalty.points` off: the pass's figure is the tier's count («13 посещения»), no «Награди», no «История», no WF, no WG. `loyalty.tiers` off: no plate on the pass, no WE. `loyalty.achievements` off: no stamp cards. `loyalty.streaks` off or the rule off: no WD, and an anchor to it lands on the page's top |
| Paused client | The pass desaturates whole (`uiFrozen`), its state line reads «Замразено»; values are never zeroed; tickets are absent; WD draws frozen; the notice `rewards-frozen` stays                                                                                                                                      |
| Guest         | Never reached (a guest has no account)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Party member  | The pass, the vouchers, WD–WG of the member's own account; NO tickets and no «Използвай» / «Код» (a companion's rewards cannot be spent on this visit, first-spec Q9)                                                                                                                                           |
| Long name     | The holder wraps to two lines of 14/600 in the foot; the state line moves under the tenure; the pass grows by the line (its ratio is a floor, `min-block-size`)                                                                                                                                                 |
| No avatar     | Not applicable: the pass carries no face                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Owes points   | The figure «−40 точки», the state line «дължи 40 точки»; primary ink, never red                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

**330 pt and the ≥ 761 dialog.** At 330 the pass is 295 × 186 (field 115, foot 71) and the state line sits under the tenure; four discs at a 73 pitch; the reward grid stays two columns (139 each), titles on two lines; the stamp card keeps 5 × 2 at 44. In the dialog (column 528) the pass keeps 355 × 224 at the leading edge and the discs sit BESIDE it as a 2 × 2; the holdings go 2-up; the rewards three across; the activity stays one column.

#### 2.2.5 The activity row, by kind

One EVENT is one row: journal entries that share a `correlationId` fold into it (a visit's seats, a level and its bonus points, a redemption and its grant). The amount is the sum of the event's point deltas; a row that moved no points has no amount. Every disc is WHITE on the grey segment (§1.1): the glyph carries the kind.

| Kind (by the event's leading effect)                                     | Glyph                                      | Title                                      | Line                                         | Trailing                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| a visit's earnings (`points`, source a visit)                            | `loyalty.activity.visit` (NEW), accent     | the visit's services                       | «5.09 · Иван»                                | «+27 т.» over the balance after           |
| a level earned (`achievement`, with or without points)                   | `loyalty.activity.level` (NEW), accent     | «Години с нас I»                           | «1.03 · постижение»                          | «+20 т.», or none                         |
| a reward redeemed (`redemption`)                                         | `loyalty.activity.redeemed` (NEW), primary | the reward's name                          | «5.09 · размяна»                             | «−250 т.» over the balance                |
| a reward received or taken back (`reward`)                               | `loyalty.activity.granted` (NEW), accent   | the reward's name                          | «5.09 · получена награда» / «отнета награда» | none                                      |
| a tier change (`tier`)                                                   | `loyalty.activity.tier` (NEW), accent      | «Статус: Злато»                            | «12.06 · от Сребро»                          | none                                      |
| the shop's points (`points`, source goodwill, service issue, correction) | `loyalty.activity.adjusted` (NEW), primary | «Бонус от салона» (the first spec's words) | the date                                     | signed amount                             |
| expired (`redemption` `expired`, points expiry)                          | `loyalty.activity.expired` (NEW), primary  | «Изтекли»                                  | the date                                     | «−40 т.»                                  |
| a reversal (`reverses` set)                                              | `loyalty.activity.reversed` (NEW), primary | «Анулирано»                                | «отменя запис от 4.09 · 5.09»                | signed amount; the reversed row is struck |

Voucher draws are not journal rows (they live on the appointment as `voucherRedemptions`): they show on the visit's row as a mark (§2.5), not here.

References: three columns with a 56–82 medal is the phone norm ([Duolingo](https://mobbin.com/screens/c305d492-71c0-433b-99e7-71309ec14278), [stoic](https://mobbin.com/screens/a0c81122-b698-4de6-86f3-5f8fb3133166), [Strava](https://mobbin.com/screens/25f88039-90f9-4175-8e8c-d78ca6d658af)); the rim ring is [Tonal](https://mobbin.com/screens/eb283ff7-7fa5-4e03-a4e0-597d6a354786) and [Tripadvisor](https://mobbin.com/screens/4e3f7f91-bf4a-4c57-b970-a9921eacfd41); records first, the emblem LARGE and the figure under it, is [Duolingo's achievements](https://mobbin.com/screens/3daecc35-91bc-45d7-975b-7d9d24024cfc) and [Strava's best efforts](https://mobbin.com/screens/d54eb1e6-1e9d-463e-9450-3e77f2b04136); chips over a grid with counts in the section heads is [adidas Running](https://mobbin.com/screens/bf2b978e-c0ec-4df9-aa77-71affc711038).

**Grid metrics.**

|                                                                                             | 330                      | 390                   | ≥ 600                        | dialog ≥ 761            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| `ui-grid [uiColumns]` (from `medalColumnsOf`, `sheet-width.ts:203`, CHANGED to these steps) | 3                        | 3                     | 4                            | 6                       |
| card padding                                                                                | 12 → `compact` + `tight` | `regular` 16          | `comfortable` 24             | 24                      |
| column gap / row gap                                                                        | `tight` / `regular`      | `compact` / `regular` | `regular` / `comfortable`    | the same                |
| tile width                                                                                  | 87                       | 102                   | a quarter of the inner width | a sixth, never under 96 |
| medal (ring / disc / stroke)                                                                | 64 / 52 / 4              | 64 / 52 / 4           | 64 / 52 / 4                  | 64 / 52 / 4             |

A tile, top to bottom: 8 · medal 64 · 8 · title `subheadline` 13 at 600, centred, two lines at most, `hyphens: auto` over the floor `overflow-wrap: anywhere` (an engine without a Bulgarian dictionary still breaks «Изследовател» inside 87 pt) · 2 · caption `footnote` 12/500 secondary, tabular · 8. No tile background: the card is the surface, and the house state layer is a rounded rectangle over the whole tile (with the ungated `:active` mirror).

**330 pt and the ≥ 761 dialog.** At 330 the chips take three rows in flow (the chip says «Бръснари»; its section keeps «Бръснари и салони»), the records stay 2 × 2 (141 wide, the label on two lines), the grids three columns of 87. From 600 the chips sit on one row, the four records in one row, the grids four columns. In the dialog the grids are six columns and the season's families eight.

#### 2.3.3 The category view (a chip other than «Всички»)

Chips FILTER, one mechanism. The records stay where they are, ABOVE the band; the chosen chip replaces everything UNDER the band with that category alone: a summary line («Майсторство · 3 от 9 спечелени», `callout` secondary), then its FULL grid, sub-sectioned as the built scope page already does («Коса и брада», «Общи», «Бръснари», «Салони», each under a `standard` heading with its own count). The body is the built `lib-achievement-category` with scope `category`, mounted in place with `embedded` (`achievements-category`); nothing is pushed. «Сезони» shows the running season's card, the events and the archive row.

Choosing a chip does not move the page: the band stays where the finger is and the body under it changes. «Покажи всички 12» chooses the chip and scrolls the BAND under the bar (its `scroll-margin-block-start` is `--modal-sheet-clear-top`). The glance and a medal detail's «Всички ›» open «Постижения» with the chip chosen and the band under the bar (§5). The chosen chip is the STORE's state: ‹ from a medal's detail returns to the same chip and scroll.

The scopes `season`, `archive` and `day` stay PUSHED pages (`achievementCategory`), their grids on the metrics above.

#### 2.3.4 «Рекорди» (`clientRecords`, NEW)

The page exists because it holds MORE than the 2 × 2: the four records of the account and five facts the card already reads for other reasons, none of which needs a new domain kind or a new read.

States: **loading** the headline, one 204 block, two groups of two 60 rows; **empty** (no record yet) `ui-empty-state` «Рекордите идват с първите посещения.»; **error** the account's notice and «Опитай пак» (the records ride the account's read, region `loyalty`); **offline** cached; **flags off** `loyalty.achievements` off: unreachable; `loyalty.streaks` off or the rule off: the «Ритъм» group is absent; **paused**, **party member**: as read; **long name**: RB wraps; **guest**: unreachable; **no avatar**: a barber without a picture draws his initials. **330**: the rows' lines wrap, the figures keep their column, «Обичайни услуги» puts its value under its title; **≥ 761**: the groups sit 2-up under the featured card.

A row that LEADS somewhere (the longest run → the history at the run's last visit; the favourite service → the history filtered by it) needs history filters that do not exist: the rows are static in this round (no state layer, no chevron) and the links are listed in §8.2. More record KINDS (a favourite shop, most services in one visit) are owner question Q9.

#### 2.3.5 States of «Постижения»

| State                   | What the page shows                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading                 | The headline at once; four 164 record blocks; two rows of chip-shaped skeletons; one grid card of six medal-shaped discs (`achievements-skeleton`)                                                                                                                                             |
| Empty                   | No level earned and nothing under way: AE shows what the book already knows («Първо посещение») or is absent; AF absent; the categories draw their dashed silhouettes (the requirement is never hidden); one line under the chips: «Първото постижение идва с първото посещение.»              |
| Error + retry           | `ui-notice` «Постиженията не се заредиха.» + «Опитай пак» (`achievements-error`, `achievements-retry`)                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Offline                 | `achievements-offline` notice; cached; every tile still opens its detail                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Flags off               | `loyalty.achievements` off: unreachable. `loyalty.streaks` off or the rule off: the «Ритъм» chip and section show only families already earned (the retired-family rule, `standing.ts:104`), and the record «Най-дълъг ритъм» is hidden (three cards: the third spans the row)                 |
| Paused client           | As read: earned levels are never taken back; nothing counts «с това посещение»                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Guest                   | Unreachable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Party member            | The member's own progress                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Long name / long titles | AB wraps. A tile title takes two lines, broken inside the word if it must («Изследовател», «Класическа подстрижка» at 330 are the test strings; the capture script asserts no horizontal overflow at 330); captions align per row                                                              |
| No avatar               | A barber family without a face draws its monogram on the disc (as built)                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Hundreds of families    | The overview stays ≈ 4 screens: records, the season, and each category two rows + «Покажи всички N»; a chip shows one category whole. At ≥ 60 families on the account, or a category over 24, `ui-search-field uiPinned` joins under the headline (the house sheet-search grammar); not before |

---

### 2.4 The medal detail (R2-H; DV-B, DV-C)

One page, six blocks, each fact once. What a game's badge page does ([Garmin](https://mobbin.com/screens/4b83593e-4774-4406-925b-188979a822fe), [GitHub](https://mobbin.com/screens/69259b1e-5d62-4ced-b583-72e104ab3e23), [Goodreads](https://mobbin.com/screens/ee47b5bb-789e-49ee-b7bf-d2958e06b258), [Forest](https://mobbin.com/screens/9d640451-86ec-4df7-a25b-5303c23a03c1), [Lyft](https://mobbin.com/screens/1a0be947-d27e-4639-8128-6ee0e81c35ad)): large art on a stage of its own, ONE requirement sentence, ONE progress figure, the reward. None prints the count twice.

#### 2.4.3 «Как се печели»: one sentence and the pictures

**The sentence** is chosen by the family's METRIC and worded around its subject; it is never assembled from a family's title by string surgery. A one-off family (`metric: null`) prints its own `BadgeSeries.requirement`.

| Metric (`MetricRef`)                                                                          | Sentence (bg)                                                                                                                        | Exclusion |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------- |
| `client.visits.completed`, `where.serviceId`                                                  | «Всяко посещение с {{service}} се брои, щом приключи.»                                                                               | yes       |
| `client.visits.completed`, `where.barberId`                                                   | «Всяко посещение при {{barber}} се брои, щом приключи.»                                                                              | yes       |
| `client.visits.completed`, `where.locationId`                                                 | «Всяко посещение в {{shop}} се брои, щом приключи.»                                                                                  | yes       |
| `client.visits.completed`, no `where`                                                         | «Всяко завършено посещение се брои.»                                                                                                 | yes       |
| `client.visits.earlyBird` · `.weekend` · `.onTime` · `.party`                                 | «Брои се всяко посещение, започнало до 10:00.» · «… в събота или неделя.» · «… за което клиентът е дошъл навреме.» · «… с компания.» | yes       |
| `client.bookings.selfService`                                                                 | «Брои се всеки час, запазен през сайта или приложението.»                                                                            | —         |
| `client.waitlist.booked`                                                                      | «Брои се всеки час, запазен от списъка на чакащите.»                                                                                 | —         |
| `client.referrals.completed`                                                                  | «Брои се всеки приятел, дошъл на първото си посещение по препоръка.»                                                                 | —         |
| `client.services.distinct` · `.barbers.distinct` · `.locations.distinct` · `.months.distinct` | «Брои се всяка различна услуга.» · «… всеки различен бръснар.» · «… всеки различен салон.» · «… всеки месец с посещение.»            | yes       |
| `client.spend.major` · `client.points.lifetime`                                               | «Брои се всяко евро, платено в салона.» · «Броят се точките от посещения и постижения.»                                              | —         |
| `client.tenure.days`                                                                          | «Брои се времето от първото посещение.»                                                                                              | —         |
| `shop.bookings.created`                                                                       | «Брои се всеки час, записан в салона.»                                                                                               | —         |
| the rhythm family (`metric: null`, value = the best run)                                      | the rule IN FORCE, `rhythmRule.fixed` / `.personal` (§6.1)                                                                           | —         |

**The pictures** (`DetailHowVm.pictures`), by the family's subject:

| Subject   | Pictures                                                                                                                                                                                            | The who line                                                                                         |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| a service | the counted services' covers with name and price (`ClientCardCatalog`): ONE cover at `16 / 9` across the card; two to four in a 2-up grid at `4 / 3`; a fifth and more fold into «+N» on the fourth | `ui-avatar-group uiControlSize="small"` (the barbers' faces) + «При всеки бръснар · във всеки салон» |
| a barber  | his picture as one cover at `4 / 3`, his name under it                                                                                                                                              | «Всяка услуга · във всеки салон»                                                                     |
| a shop    | the shop's cover at `16 / 9`, its name under it                                                                                                                                                     | the faces + «При всеки бръснар · всяка услуга»                                                       |
| none      | no picture                                                                                                                                                                                          | the faces + «При всеки бръснар · във всеки салон»                                                    |

A cover is a picture, not a control: the page's one verb is in the dock. `DetailHowVm.steps` is reserved and empty in this round (DV-B).

#### 2.4.4 States

| State                  | What the page shows                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading                | The stage as one 176 block with a 128 disc skeleton, two lines; one 140 block; one 356 block (`achievements-detail-skeleton`)                                                                                     |
| Not started            | The whole page: the dashed silhouette on the stage, «Майсторство · още не е започнато», «0 от 3», every level of the ladder a dashed silhouette, the first level's words primary. The requirement is never hidden |
| Complete               | DE's figure is the last threshold, «Всички нива»; the dock has no verb; the hero's ring is full; every row of DH is a full medal                                                                                  |
| One-off                | DE's figure is the state («С това посещение», or the date); DH is one «Награда» row; a yearly one lists the years it was earned in DD                                                                             |
| Retired family         | DD «Вече не се присъжда.» (as built); DF and the verb absent                                                                                                                                                      |
| Error + retry, offline | As §2.3.5; offline the verb is absent                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Flags off              | `loyalty.achievements` off: unreachable                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Paused client          | The verb is absent; the rest reads                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Party member           | The member's own progress; the verb adds the service to THAT person's seat                                                                                                                                        |
| Long title             | DC wraps to two lines of 28 («Оформяне на брада»); the stage does not move                                                                                                                                        |
| No art                 | The hero disc draws the monogram or the emblem at the disc's scale; DF has no cover and keeps the sentence and the who line                                                                                       |
| Guest                  | Unreachable                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

**330 pt and the ≥ 761 dialog.** At 330 the stage is 295 × 176 and its motif discs are dropped (the spotlight stays); the covers are 117 × 88; the ladder's words wrap; DJ's three tiles are 87 wide. In the dialog DE sits beside DF's first half (2-up), the ladder beside the visits, and DJ shows six tiles in its one row.

### 2.5 «Посещения» with `lib-visits-browser` (R2-I; DV-D)

The test of this page is one sentence: put it beside `/account/appointments` at rest (`v2/account/appointments.png`) and it is the same screen. There: the title, the count under it, the pills ≈ 80 under the title, the list. Here: the title, the facts under it, the pills 72 under the title, the list.

```

The body under HC is the SAME component the client's `/account/appointments` mounts: both screens change together from now on. The staff page adds what only staff see (the facts, money, status words, «Този час», the marks, paging) through inputs, never through a second list.

#### 2.5.2 Region spec

Body: `lib-client-history` (`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-history/`).

| #     | element         | DS component + inputs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | type             | ink                            | space                  | size                          | data source                                                                                                                               | copy bg / en                                                                               | a11y                                                                                                   | test id                                                                                                                               |
| ----- | --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------ | ---------------------- | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| HA    | title           | `ui-sheet-headline` > `h2 uiFont="largeTitle"`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `largeTitle`     | primary                        | —                      | —                             | —                                                                                                                                         | «Посещения» / "Visits"                                                                     | h2                                                                                                     | `history-title`                                                                                                                       |

| HC    | the second line | `p uiFont="subheadline" uiForegroundStyle="secondary"`, inside the headline's text (the headline's "one line" becomes "the quiet lines": §3.3)                                                                                                                                                    | `subheadline` 13 | secondary; the sum primary/500 | 4                      | 1–2 lines                     | as built (`historyFacts`); the spend ‹admin› only; absent when it has nothing to say                                                      | «Без неявявания и откази от клиента» · «похарчени общо 492,50 €»                           | text                                                                                                   | `history-facts`, `history-note`, `history-spend`                                                                                      |
| HF–HJ | the browser     | `lib-visits-browser [sections] perspective="staff" listStyle="insetGrouped" bleed="container" [state] [paging] testIdPrefix="history"` (NEW, §3.11), with `lib-visit-filter visitPillsAction perspective="staff" testIdPrefix="history-filter" [offered]` projected into the rail's trailing slot | the row's own    | the row's own                  | 24                     | pills 36; rows 68             | `visitsRows` / `visitsSections` with `grain: 'month'` (§7.3) over `ClientCardStore`'s pages                                               | month labels; the rows' words as built                                                     | pills: a navigation landmark «Месеци»; the funnel a menu button named «Филтър»; rows static list items | `history-pills`, `history-pill`, `history-month-{YYYY-MM}`, `history-filter*`, `history-upcoming`, `history-row-{id}`, `history-more` |

**The staff row** is the client's row plus the staff's fact. The face stays TRAILING and spans both lines, exactly as on the client's page: that is the sameness. The row's grid is `[tile 48] [text 1fr] [face 48]`; the face column is **reserved on every row**, face or not (a visit cut by several draws none), so the second line's trailing facts end on ONE vertical line, 48 + the row's trailing padding from the card's edge, and the amounts read as a column.

- First line: the services, `body` 15/500.
- Second line, leading: the fact line (mono `footnote`: «Център · Иван», «днес, 10:00 · Иван»).
- Second line, trailing (the staff's fact, at most one): the amount paid (`formatMoney`, `callout` 14/600, primary, tabular; struck and secondary on a cancelled hour), or the status in words beside its glyph («Не дойде», «Заявен»), or the capsule «Този час».
- Marks stand before the fact: photos counted (`client.photos`), a discount (`promo.coupon`), and NEW a voucher (`promo.voucher`, «платено с ваучер») when `Appointment.voucherRedemptions` is not empty (Fresha adopt-now N5).
- Widths at 390: the row's inner width 323, the tile 48 + 12, the face 48: the text column is 215. «Фейд · Оформяне на брада» (≈ 175 at 15/500) holds one line; the second line gives the fact line ≈ 120 and the trailing fact the rest.

**The funnel** is not a heading's action: there is no heading to hang it on. It is the LAST thing of the pill rail, pinned at its trailing end (the rail scrolls under a short fade before it), `small` (36), icon-only, bordered, wearing the accent dot while a filter bites (§1.3).

**The section detail** («3 посещения») prints only when a month holds two or more worked visits; a one-row month is its heading alone.

**Rows stay static** (the first spec's R7): no hover, no press, no chevron. They look like the client's tappable rows; whether a staff row should open that visit is owner question **Q6**, asked at the first review, BEFORE this page is built (§7.6). A yes turns the row into `button[uiListRow]` (one input of the browser, `interactive`) and adds the editor's hop; nothing else on this page changes.

#### 2.5.3 States

| State                | What the page shows                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading              | The headline; one line of `ui-skeleton` under it; three pill skeletons; two groups of two 68 rows                                                           |
| Empty                | 0 visits on record and none upcoming: HB «Първо посещение»; the list holds this visit under «Предстоящи»                                                    |
| Filtered empty       | Under the rail: «Няма посещения за този филтър.» + «Покажи всички» (`history-filter-empty`, `history-filter-clear`); the pills are absent, the funnel stays |
| Error + retry        | The list's own notice «Посещенията не се заредиха.» + «Опитай пак»; a failed «Покажи още» keeps the rows and offers the retry in the foot                   |
| Offline              | `history-offline` notice; cached pages; HI absent while a cursor is left                                                                                    |
| Flags off            | None applies (the history rides no loyalty flag)                                                                                                            |
| Paused client        | As read                                                                                                                                                     |
| Guest                | Unreachable (no uid to read by)                                                                                                                             |
| Party member         | Reads by the member's own uid (`subjectUserIds`); the amount is the member's share; the quote only on visits the member booked                              |
| Long name            | Does not apply: the page names no person (the bar's way back does)                                                                                          |
| No avatar            | A barber without a picture: the avatar's initials in the trailing face                                                                                      |
| 19 months of history | 19 pills; the rail scrolls, the current pill follows the scroll (the client page has the same); the funnel stays pinned                                     |

#### 2.5.4 330 pt and the ≥ 761 dialog

At 330 HB wraps to two balanced lines; the text column is ≈ 155: a two-service title wraps to two lines (`uiLabelWraps`), the row grows to ≈ 86 and the face stays centred on it. In the dialog HB and HC share one line, the pills bleed to the dialog's inset, the list is one column.

---

### 2.6 The admin page «Ритъм» (`/admin/loyalty/rhythm`, R2-J)

A routed page, not a sheet: NEW lib `libs/features/admin/loyalty` (`@creativo/features/admin/loyalty`, project `admin-loyalty`, tags `scope:app`, `type:feature`, prefix `lib`, scaffolded by K4 in W0, §7.1), component `AdminRhythm` (`lib-admin-rhythm`), `providers: [AdminRhythmStore]`. Route: `canActivate: [activeGuard, rolesGuard('admin', 'sysadmin')]` (the pair `isAdmin()` names, `apps/web/src/app/app.routes.ts:112-122`) and `canDeactivate: [adminRhythmCanDeactivate]` (NEW, §3.17: the app's first); `/admin/loyalty` redirects to it.

**What the owner asked for is what the page shows** (F7: "on, off, in what interval of appointments / actions it gets saved"): the rule and its switch, what counts, the interval, the grace. The other eleven controls (protections, excuses, what a miss does) are ONE TAP away behind three value rows that state their value, the iOS Settings grammar. Same domain, a third of the scroll. It is built ONLY from DS controls: no chips as pickers, no native inputs, no hard-coded strings (the faults of `admin-programs.html`).
