References: a switch leading a group with a quiet footnote under it ([Toggl Track](https://mobbin.com/screens/c58f9884-949a-4f28-b54b-be8bf22d180e), [GitHub](https://mobbin.com/screens/e4cad526-3b71-49b6-b0bf-ff5e24a47d7f)); a before → after review ([Arcade](https://mobbin.com/screens/b850abdb-2418-47b3-adaf-eac362756c6d), [Patreon](https://mobbin.com/screens/e1bb46f2-6fe4-47f8-b529-f4fa5464409a)); an effective date as its own step ([Deel](https://mobbin.com/screens/70753431-9dc3-4c98-873f-a3273127ff34)). No loyalty back-office surveyed ships a preview (Square tells the merchant to "make sure your customers and cashiers understand the impact"); this page does.

#### 2.6.1a The five sheets

A routed page has no page stack, so nothing here "pushes". A secondary editor is a `ui-modal-sheet` (the house form: a yes/no question is `ui-alert`, a FORM is `ui-modal-sheet`), one component `lib-admin-rhythm-sheet [kind]` with a `@switch` on its kind. Each edits the page's DRAFT, never the rule: its dock holds one `borderedProminent` «Готово» that closes it; ✕, the scrim and Escape close it the same way (there is nothing to discard inside a sheet: the page's «Запази» is the one write). The focus returns to the row that opened it.

| Sheet (`kind`)                | Opened by                | Holds                                                                                                                                                                                               | The row's value reads                                            |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `services` «Услуги»           | «Услуги, които се броят» | `ui-sheet-headline` + `ui-search-field uiPinned` (the house sheet-search grammar) over the catalogue's services as `button[uiListRow] [uiSelected]`, grouped by category; at least one stays chosen | «3 услуги»                                                       |
| `protections` «Защити»        | «Защити»                 | P8, P8a, P8b, P8c and the footnote                                                                                                                                                                  | «До 2 в запас»; off: «Изключени»                                 |
| `excuses` «Извинени пропуски» | «Извинени пропуски»      | P9's four switches with the inline warning, P10 and its limit                                                                                                                                       | «3 от 4»; with the client's pause on: «3 от 4 · пауза до 90 дни» |
| `afterMiss` «След пропуск»    | «След пропуск»           | P12, P11 and its two menus                                                                                                                                                                          | «Започва отначало»; «Продължава оттам»                           |
| `day` «Влизане в сила»        | P13's «Избери ден»       | `ui-month-picker [uiMin]="earliestDay()"` (the day IS the answer: a tap chooses and closes)                                                                                                         | the chosen day, «от 12.10»                                       |

#### 2.6.2 Every control

`draft` below is `AdminRhythmStore.draft()`, a `StreakDefinitionProps` (§4.1). Every row is a `ui-list-row uiSize="regular"` in a `ul[uiListGroup]` unless a readout card is named. Test ids are `admin-rhythm-{id}`; the three value rows are `admin-rhythm-row-{protections·excuses·afterMiss}` and the sheets `admin-rhythm-sheet-{kind}`. **Where**: P1–P7 and P13–P14 on the page; P2a in the sheet `services`; P8–P8c in `protections`; P9–P10 in `excuses`; P11–P12 in `afterMiss`. **Clamping**: `ui-unit-field` has `uiMin` and no `uiMax` and does not clamp (`unit-field.ts:74-92`), so the STORE clamps on `(uiValueChange)` (`AdminRhythmStore.patch`) and hands the clamped value back. **Units** are never literals: `[uiUnit]` takes `rhythmRule.days.*` / `rhythmRule.weeks.*` through the words.

| P11 | repair              | «Връщане с посещение» + «В рамките на» + «Пъти годишно»                                               | `button[uiSwitch]` + `ui-choice-menu` 7 · 14 · 21 · 30 + `ui-choice-menu` 1 · 2 · 3                                                                    | on · 14 · 1                                                                    | `repair`                                        | `repair`, `repair-window`, `repair-per-year`                   |
| P13 | effective from      | «От» / "From"                                                                                         | `ui-choice-menu` «От утре» · «От 1-во число» · «Избери ден» (the third opens the sheet `day`)                                                          | ≥ the earliest day the domain allows (§4.1) · tomorrow                         | `effectiveFrom`                                 | `effective-from`                                               |
| P14 | existing runs       | «Досегашните ритми» / "Existing runs"                                                                 | a static value «Запазват се» in this round: «Преизчисляват се» needs the engine and is NOT offered (absent, never disabled)                            | `grandfather`                                                                  | `transition`                                    | `transition`                                                   |

Fixed by ethics, never a control (rhythm-admin §3.2): a protection is automatic and free; dates, never countdowns; the best run never decreases; a no-show never breaks the rhythm by itself; an account pause freezes the rhythm.

#### 2.6.4 Behaviour: the draft, the confirmation, the receipt

- **The form is a draft.** Nothing is written until «Запази». The dock appears only when the draft differs from the rule in force AND is valid. Leaving with a draft (the back chip, the menu, the browser's back) asks first: the route's `adminRhythmCanDeactivate` calls the component's `confirmLeave(): Promise<boolean>`, which raises `ui-alert` «Отхвърли промените?» (the destructive answer, and the safe one last, bold, what Escape means). Without a draft it answers `true` at once.
- **The rule sentence and the sample track redraw on every change.** The preview recomputes by itself 600 ms after the last change (an auto-action; there is no «Преизчисли»). The clients' stats are READ ONCE per visit to the page and kept in the store (five pages of 200 at most); a recompute folds what is already in memory and reads nothing. Past 1 000 clients the card says «от първите 1 000 клиенти».
- **An invalid combination cannot be reached by the controls**: the grace slider's maximum follows the interval, the store clamps what a unit field hands it. The domain's errors are the second line of defence and surface as one `ui-notice uiTone="destructive"` naming the field.
- **Switching off** collapses MD–MG and leaves MC, ML and, with a draft, MJ–MK.
- **Publish.** «Запази» classifies the change (`StreakDefinition.changeFrom`, §4.1):

| Direction           | When                                                                                                                                           | Confirmation                                                                                                             | After                                                                                                    |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `looser`, `neutral` | a longer interval, more grace, more protections, an excuse added, a longer repair window, the hold widened, `restart → hold`; or the cue alone | none: it saves                                                                                                           | the receipt `ui-toast` «Правилото е запазено · от 30.09» with «Отмени» (withdraws the scheduled version) |
| `stricter`, `mixed` | the reverse of any of those, or a narrower qualifier                                                                                           | `ui-alert` «Запази по-строго правило?», the preview's counts as its message, the verb «Запази» as the button, no default | the receipt, without undo                                                                                |
| `switch`            | `enabled` flips                                                                                                                                | `ui-alert` «Изключи ритъма?» / «Включи ритъма?» with what happens to the runs                                            | the receipt                                                                                              |

- **Scheduling.** A version whose effective day is later than tomorrow is SCHEDULED: MC shows the capsule «От 1.10» and a second sentence «От 1.10: на всеки 5 седмици»; ML gains the withdraw row. Publishing again while one is scheduled replaces it.
- **«Преизчисляват се»** is refused by the server (`recomputeNeedsEngine`) until the engine ships, so the page does not offer it.

#### 2.6.5 States

| State               | What the page shows                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Loading             | MC as one block; each group as its rows' skeleton (`admin-rhythm-skeleton`)                                                                                                                                                 |
| Empty               | No rule document yet: the page opens on `StreakDefinition.default()` as the draft, MC says «Още няма правило. Това са препоръчаните стойности.», «Запази» is present                                                        |
| Error + retry       | `ui-notice` «Правилото не се зареди.» + «Опитай пак» (`admin-rhythm-retry`)                                                                                                                                                 |
| Save refused        | `stale`: `ui-notice` «Правилото беше променено от друг. Виж новото.», the draft kept; `invalid`: the field's notice; `tooEarly`: the effective day moves to the earliest allowed and says so; `notAdmin`: the refusal toast |
| Offline             | The dock is absent and a notice says why; the form still reads                                                                                                                                                              |
| Flags off           | `loyalty.streaks` off: the page works (the rule can be prepared before the rollout) and MC carries a neutral `ui-notice` «Ритъмът още не е включен за приложението.»                                                        |
| Preview unavailable | With «избрани услуги»: MK says «Прегледът не е наличен за избрани услуги.» and the save still works                                                                                                                         |
| Not an admin        | The route guard refuses; the menu has no entry                                                                                                                                                                              |

The client's states (paused, guest, party member, long name, no avatar) do not apply to this page. **330**: a row whose label and value do not share a line puts the value under the label, still trailing-aligned; the sliders and their readouts keep the full column. **≥ 761**: one column at `--sys-container-content`, the sheets present as centred dialogs (the sheet's own adaptive rule); from 1024 MC and MK are sticky beside the form.

---

## 3. Component specifications

### 3.0 Placement and the dependency direction

`patterns` import `@creativo/ui/icons`, `@creativo/ui/modifiers`, other patterns and, NEW, `@creativo/ui/layout` (a leaf that imports nothing: checked, `libs/ui/layout/src/lib/**` has no `@creativo/ui/*` import), NEVER `controls` (checked: `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/**` imports only `icons` and `modifiers` today). `controls` may import `patterns`, `layout`, `icons`, `modifiers`. A pattern that needs a control (a badge, an avatar, a button) takes it PROJECTED. Feature libs import anything in `libs/ui/*`. A feature NEVER imports a domain lib (`eslint.config.mjs:149-157`): what it needs of the domain it takes from the application barrel, re-exported by name (§7.3).

Graph (no cycles): `staff/dashboard → staff/client-card → shared/visits → ui/*`; `client/appointments → shared/visits`; `client/account → shared/visits` (NEW edge; all three are `scope:app`, `type:feature`); `admin/loyalty → shared/visits, application/engagement, application/booking, ui/*` (NEW lib). `shared/visits` imports neither consumer. The showcase imports only `libs/ui/*`. Every NEW edge needs its project reference (the libs are `composite`): K4 writes them (§7.1).

Every piece keeps the first spec's conventions (§5.0): `ViewEncapsulation.None`, `OnPush`, host class `ui-<name>`, state as `data-*` on the host, signal inputs with the `ui` prefix and SwiftUI names, CSS in `@layer sys-components` with tokens only, `font:` shorthands followed by `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`, reduced-motion branches, consumers own `data-testid`, a Vitest spec beside each piece, a showcase page `apps/showcase/src/app/pages/controls/<slug>/`. `uiLabel` is always the accessible name.

### 3.1 `ui-quick-actions`: discs (R2-B; ES-1)

|                         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selectors               | `ui-quick-actions`; `a[uiQuickAction], button[uiQuickAction]` (unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Inputs                  | `ui-quick-actions`: `uiLabel` (required), `uiColumns = 4`. The action: `uiIcon: UiIconName` (required), **NEW** `uiProminence: 'standard' \| 'accent' = 'standard'` → `data-prominence`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Template (the action)   | `<span class="ui-quick-action__disc"><ui-icon class="ui-quick-action__glyph" [uiName] uiScale="large" /></span><span class="ui-quick-action__label"><ng-content /></span>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Layout                  | the run: `grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--ui-quick-actions-columns), minmax(0, var(--ui-quick-actions-pitch)))`, `justify-content: center`, no gap (the pitch is the rhythm). The action: a column, `align-items: center`, gap `compact` (8), `padding-block: tight`, `background: none`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Sizes                   | disc `--ui-quick-action-disc` = `--control-size-large` (52), `--control-radius-capsule`; glyph 24; caption `footnote` 12/500, ONE line (`white-space: nowrap; text-overflow: ellipsis`); pitch `--ui-quick-actions-pitch` = 5.5rem (88), shrinking equally under it (73 at 330 with four verbs)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Fill and ink            | `standard`: disc `var(--ui-quick-action-fill, var(--sys-color-fill))`, glyph `var(--ui-quick-action-ink, var(--sys-color-foreground))`. `accent`: disc `color-mix(in srgb, var(--sys-color-accent) var(--sys-alpha-tint), transparent)`, glyph `var(--sys-color-accent)`. Caption always `var(--sys-color-foreground)`. One `accent` per run: a second one warns in development                                                                                                                                                        |
| The owner's choice (Q2) | The two custom properties are the WHOLE difference between R2-B's row and the owner's "before". The showcase page draws both rows one over the other, captioned «Неутрални (R2-B)» and «Оцветени (както преди)»: the second sets `--ui-quick-action-fill` to the accent wash and `--ui-quick-action-ink` to the accent on the run, and draws its promoted verb as a SOLID accent disc with a white glyph (a mark, rule 5). If he picks the second, D1 moves the two defaults and adds the solid rule for `accent`; no consumer changes |
| States                  | The state layer is the DISC's `::after`, driven by the host: `@media (hover: hover) { .ui-quick-action:hover .ui-quick-action__disc::after { background: var(--sys-state-layer-hover) } }`; ungated `.ui-quick-action:active .ui-quick-action__disc::after { background: var(--sys-state-layer-press) }` and the disc scales to `--sys-press-scale-control`. `:focus-visible`: the ring round the DISC. The host loses `data-interactive`                                                                                              |
| A11y                    | `role="group"` + `aria-label`; each action a native link or button whose name begins with its visible word. The accent glyph on its own wash is 2.62:1: legal because the caption names the verb (SC 1.4.11 binds a graphic that is the only carrier)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Custom props            | `--ui-quick-actions-columns`, NEW `--ui-quick-actions-pitch`, NEW `--ui-quick-action-disc`, NEW `--ui-quick-action-fill`, NEW `--ui-quick-action-ink`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Removed                 | the tile face: `min-block-size: var(--control-size-tile)`, the regular corner, the fill on the host, the two-line clamp. `--control-size-tile` stays for `ui-choice-tiles` alone; the comments at `tokens.css:89-94` and `choice-tiles.css:10-18` stop naming quick actions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Consumers               | `client-card.html:190-215`, `client-loyalty.html` (the wallet's discs), `client-appointments.html:424-487` (the client's visit sheet returns to discs too), the showcase. No input is removed, so every consumer compiles unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Showcase                | `apps/showcase/src/app/pages/controls/quick-actions/` re-written: four verbs, three verbs, a promoted verb, 330, and the two fills of Q2                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### 3.2 `ui-section-header`: the action stays inside the column (R2-K), and the heading's rung

`libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/section-header/section-header.css`. The rules at `:101-121` (the negative inline margins for `regular`, `small`, `large`, icon-only) and `:155-157` (the −52 block margin) are DELETED and replaced by:

```css
/* EVERY `increased` heading is the small rung tall, action or not: one
   rhythm, and nothing has to overhang to keep it. */
.ui-section-header[data-header-prominence='increased'] {
  min-block-size: var(--control-size-small);
}

/* A plain action hugs its label INSIDE the column: the trailing glyph's own
   side bearing is its trailing padding, so the capsule ends on the edge the
   cards end on and nothing paints in the gutter. */
.ui-section-header[data-header-prominence]
  > [uisectionaction].ui-button[data-button-style='plain'] {
  position: relative; /* the hit slop below; the ring above the card */
  z-index: 1;
  margin: 0;
}
.ui-section-header[data-header-prominence]
  > [uisectionaction].ui-button[data-button-style='plain']:has(
    > .ui-icon:last-child
  ) {
  padding-inline-end: 0;
}
/* The 44 floor is a TARGET, never paint. */
.ui-section-header[data-header-prominence]
  > [uisectionaction].ui-button::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: calc((var(--control-size-small) - var(--control-size-regular)) / 2) 0;
}
```

|                              |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Inputs                       | unchanged (`uiHeaderProminence`, `uiIcon`, `uiPinned`, `uiAlignment`, `uiOnMedia`, `uiEyebrowTone`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| The heading's rung           | `.ui-section-header[data-header-prominence='increased'] > [uititle]` (`:127-130`) becomes `font: var(--sys-font-title3)` (20/600), from `headline` (18/600): a heading now outranks the 18/600 headline of the card under it (§2.0). `increased` is used by the client stack and the showcase only (checked: four client-card templates and `section-header.page.html`), and by the admin page of this round |
| Contract for consumers       | the action is `uiControlSize="small"` (36). A `regular` or `large` plain action in the slot warns in development                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Measured (ds-defects V1)     | box 54.84 × 36, 0 past the column, 1.70 clear of the card below; text and chevron do not move from today's rest state                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Focus                        | `outline` 2 + offset 2: the ring's outer edge is 4 past the box, inside the sheet's 17.6 inset, above the card (`z-index: 1`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Also here                    | the accent eyebrow's ink (§1.3)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Consumers to move to `small` | `client-card.html:290, 362, 423` and its placeholder `:96`; the achievements and detail headings; `apps/showcase/.../section-header.page.html:125` (its prose at `:110-113` is rewritten). Until they move (W2) a `regular` action still renders, inside the column, 44 tall                                                                                                                                 |

### 3.3 `ui-sheet-headline`: the hero layout (R2-C), the stage, and two hooks of the sheet

|                      |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector             | `ui-sheet-headline`; bundle `UI_SHEET_HEADLINE = [UiSheetHeadline, UiHeadlineMedia, UiHeadlinePlate]` (the third is NEW)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Inputs               | `uiFolded`, `uiMediaTone: 'warning' \| null` (existing). **NEW** `uiHeadlineStyle: 'standard' \| 'hero' = 'standard'` → `data-headline-style`. **NEW** `uiMediaValue: number \| null = null` (0 … 1): the ring round the face. **NEW** `uiMediaStage = false` (`booleanAttribute`, hero only) → `data-media-stage`: the face stands on a stage                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Slots                | default: the `h2 uiSheetLargeTitle` and at most TWO quiet lines (was one: the history's facts take two, §2.5); `[uiHeadlineMedia]` (ONE face: `ui-avatar` or `ui-medal`, `extraLarge` in the hero, `large` in the trailing layout); **NEW** `[uiHeadlinePlate]` (one `span[uiBadge uiOnMedia]`, hero only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Template             | `<div class="__body"><div class="__text"><ng-content /></div><span class="__media" aria-hidden="true">@if (uiMediaStage()) { <span class="__spot"></span><span class="__motif"></span><span class="__motif"></span> } @if (ring()) { <ui-gauge class="__ring" uiGaugeStyle="circularCapacity" uiControlSize="extraLarge" … /> }<ng-content select="[uiHeadlineMedia]" /><ng-content select="[uiHeadlinePlate]" /></span></div>`. The PLATE is projected INSIDE the media, so whatever happens to the face happens to it. DOM order stays title first; the hero moves the media with `order: -1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Hero layout          | the host rises: `margin-block-start: calc(-1 * var(--ui-sheet-headline-rise, 0px))`. Body: one column, `justify-items: center`, `text-align: center`. Media box 128 (the ring box, kept even without a ring, so nothing moves when a flag flips). Plate: `position: absolute`, centred on the ring box's bottom edge (half over it). Text: `min-block-size: 0`, gap `tight` (4), 16 under the plate (or under the media without one)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| The rise             | `--ui-sheet-headline-rise` is the consumer's. The glance sets `calc(var(--modal-sheet-chrome-height) - var(--sys-space-comfortable))` (37 of 61: the ring's top is 35 under the grabber, DV-G; `v2/ds-defects/hero/light-390-h2-own-timeline-y000.png` against `…h3-raised-y000.png`). The medal detail sets none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| The ring             | `uiMediaTone="warning"` wins: a FULL ring in `--sys-color-warning`. Else `uiMediaValue`: track `--sys-color-separator`, arc `--sys-color-accent`, stroke 6, gap 2, from 12 o'clock, clockwise, round caps; a zero value draws the track alone. A projected `ui-medal` wears its own ring: the consumer leaves `uiMediaValue` null                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| The stage            | `uiMediaStage`: the media is the column wide and `ring box + 2 × comfortable` tall (176), radius `--control-radius-prominent`, `background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--sys-color-accent) var(--sys-alpha-tint), transparent)`. `.__spot`: a disc `ring box + 2 × 12` (152) in `--sys-color-surface-elevated`, centred under the ring, so the arc is read on white (3.04:1). `.__motif` × 2: the pass's discs (220 and 140) in `color-mix(in srgb, var(--sys-color-accent) 10%, transparent)`, in the trailing 40 %, clipped by the radius, dropped in a container narrower than 20rem. No gradient, no motion                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| The media's own exit | hero only, in `sheet-headline.css`, inside `@supports (animation-timeline: view())` and `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference`: `.ui-sheet-headline[data-headline-style='hero'][data-media] .ui-sheet-headline__media { view-timeline-name: --ui-sheet-headline-media; view-timeline-inset: calc(var(--modal-sheet-chrome-height, var(--control-size-large)) - var(--ui-sheet-headline-rise, 0px)) auto; animation-timeline: --ui-sheet-headline-media; animation-range: exit 0% exit 55%; }`. The rule is MORE SPECIFIC than the header's (`sheet-header.css:196-201`), whose selector and `animation` it leaves alone: it only re-aims the timeline and the range. So `sheet-header.css`, `sheet-header.spec.ts:294-300` and `modal-sheet.spec.ts:165-176`, which pin the header's selector, do not change. The inset is the chrome LESS the rise: `exit 0%` is the box's top reaching the point it stood at, or the bar's bottom edge when it did not rise. At 390 the glance's face starts to leave at 16 px of scroll and is gone at 86; the stage starts at 16 and is gone at 113; the compact title arrives from ≈ 150 and ≈ 223. The plate is inside the media: at no scroll position is a plate, a face or a stage at full ink under a name in the bar |
| Fallback             | no scroll-driven animations, or reduced motion: `.ui-sheet__surface:has(.ui-sheet-header[data-collapsed]) .ui-sheet-headline[data-headline-style='hero'] .ui-sheet-headline__media { opacity: 0; }` (NEW rule in `sheet-headline.css`, outside the `@supports` block's motion branch): the face, the plate and the stage leave with the header's flip. No rule did this for the trailing layout (checked: `sheet-headline.css`, `sheet-header.css:93-109`); there the chrome's fade covers a 52 disc, here the object is 128                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Pointer              | the media is `pointer-events: none`: a drag that starts on the face still moves the sheet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| A11y                 | the media, its ring and its plate are `aria-hidden`; the consumer says the plate's words and the ring's meaning in a visually hidden `span` after the quiet line (§2.1 A3′)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Custom props         | NEW `--ui-sheet-headline-rise`; `--ui-sheet-headline-media-gap`, `--ui-sheet-headline-gap` (existing)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Sizes                | hero: face 112 (`extraLarge`), ring box 128; trailing: face 52, unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Trailing layout      | untouched, still documented, used by no page of the client stack                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Showcase             | `controls/sheet-headline/`: the hero with a ring and a plate; with the warning ring; with a medal on the stage; with a three-line name; with two quiet lines; at 330                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

**`ui-sheet-header`** (`sheet-header.ts:185-196`): its private `findScroller()` becomes a call of `nearestScroller(this.elementRef.nativeElement)` from `@creativo/ui/layout` (NEW, the original moved up, not copied: `lib-visits-browser` needs the same walk). `export function nearestScroller(from: Element): HTMLElement | null`, the first ancestor whose computed `overflow-y` is `auto` or `scroll`. `sheet-header.spec.ts` keeps its cases.

**`ui-modal-sheet`** (`modal-sheet.css:131-134`): the rule that gives a page with a pinned head a fade that keeps to the bar gains one selector, `.modal-sheet__scroll:has([data-sheet-fade='tight'])`, beside `:has(.ui-section-header[data-pinned])`. A body whose first object is a coloured surface asks for it with `data-sheet-fade="tight"` on its host (the wallet: without it the bar's 3 rem white fade switches on over the orange pass at the first pixel of scroll, since a bar without a large title collapses on any scroll, `sheet-header.ts:110-114`). `modal-sheet.spec.ts` gains one case.

### 3.4 `ui-coupon-card` (white ticket, accent figure) and `ui-occasion-band` (R2-D)

**The ticket.**

|                          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selectors                | `button[uiCouponCard], article[uiCouponCard]` (unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Inputs                   | unchanged: `uiTitle` (required), `uiValue` (required, `string \| null`), `uiCode`, `uiDetail`, `uiAction`, `uiApplied`, `uiUnavailable`, `uiTestId`. `uiAction` (the WRITTEN call) is **deprecated**: it still renders as today, the client stack stops passing it in W2, the editor's discount page in W3, and it is removed in W4                                                                                           |
| Slots                    | `[uiCouponGlyph]`, `[uiCouponBadge]`, `[uiCouponAction]` (existing); **NEW** `ui-occasion-band` (the first child; the card finds it with `contentChild(UiOccasionBand)` and stamps `data-occasion`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Surface                  | the paper is a NEW part `.ui-coupon-card__paper` (absolute, `inset: 0`, behind the content): it takes the background, the radius and the notch MASK; the host takes `filter: drop-shadow(var(--ui-coupon-card-shadow, var(--sys-elevation-raised-drop)))`, so the lift follows the notched silhouette (a `box-shadow` is clipped by the mask). `--ui-coupon-card-surface` now DEFAULTS to `var(--sys-color-surface-elevated)` |
| `filter` is ONE property | `.ui-coupon-card[data-unavailable]` (`coupon-card.css:119-122`) sets `filter: saturate(0.35)`, which would silently replace the lift. It becomes `filter: drop-shadow(var(--ui-coupon-card-shadow, var(--sys-elevation-raised-drop))) saturate(0.35)`; the spec pins both functions in the unavailable rule                                                                                                                   |
| The glyph                | a 44 disc (`--control-size-regular`), accent wash, the projected glyph 24 in `--sys-color-promo`; the badge's ring follows the surface hook as today                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| The figure               | `.ui-coupon-card__value`: `font: var(--sys-font-title2)` (24/700), `color: var(--sys-color-promo)`, drawn ABOVE the title (`order: -1`); the title is `callout` 14/600, or `headline` 18/600 when there is no value                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| The call                 | EVERY ticket that offers something wears the SAME call: a decorative `span[uiButton uiButtonStyle="borderedProminent" uiControlSize="small" aria-hidden="true"]` projected into `[uiCouponAction]` (the house form for button chrome inside a larger tappable surface). The ticket is the control; the pill is its dress. A ticket that offers nothing here (the wallet's, a voucher) projects none                           |
| With an occasion band    | the tear and the notches move to the SEAM between the band and the body (`--ui-coupon-card-tear: var(--ui-occasion-band-height)` from the top); the foot loses its own tear; the body has no glyph disc (the band's disc carries it)                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| States                   | rest, hover / press (the house layer over the paper), focus (the inset ring, as today), applied, unavailable (muted whole)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| A11y                     | unchanged: the button form is one control named by the consumer's `aria-label`; the article form is pressed only on what it projects                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Consumers                | the glance E and BD, the wallet's tickets and vouchers, the editor's discount page (its tickets turn white too)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Showcase                 | **NEW** `controls/coupon-card/`: offer, offer without a value, a voucher, applied, unavailable (lifted AND desaturated), with the band, on a dark sheet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

**The band.**

|                 |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector        | `ui-occasion-band` (NEW)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Inputs          | `uiTitle: string` (required)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Slots           | `[uiOccasionGlyph]` (a `ui-icon`, drawn 24 in the accent on a 44 white disc); `[uiOccasionDetail]` (one `span[uiBadge uiOnMedia]` under the title: kept for a consumer that needs it, unused in this round, DV-E)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Layout          | `block-size: var(--ui-occasion-band-height)` (6rem, 96), padding `regular`, the disc leading, the title beside it, vertically centred                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Ink             | surface `var(--sys-color-accent)`; title `title2` 24/700 in `var(--sys-color-on-accent)` (3.04:1, large text), one line. The disc is white in BOTH themes (`var(--sys-color-on-accent)`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Flecks          | FOURTEEN `aria-hidden` spans, STATIC, laid over the WHOLE band on a fixed map (percent positions, so the map scales): 6 pt dots, 4 × 12 bars at four angles, 12 pt four-point sparks. Two inks at FULL strength, alternating: `var(--sys-color-on-accent)` (white) and `var(--sys-color-media-canvas)` (near-black). The title's zone (the band's height from the disc's leading edge to the title's end, measured by the text column itself: the flecks sit in a layer UNDER it and the text column paints the solid accent behind its own box) stays solid. No gradient, no emoji, no motion. In a container narrower than 22.5rem (360) the map keeps EIGHT marks (the four corners' and the four nearest the trailing edge); it never drops to none |
| Standalone      | fully rounded (`--control-radius-prominent`), `--sys-elevation-raised`; the consumer adds `role="note"`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Inside a ticket | the top corners only; the ticket's mask cuts the notches at its foot                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Custom props    | `--ui-occasion-band-height`, `--ui-occasion-band-surface`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### 3.5 `ui-pass-card`: the loyalty pass (NEW)

THE PASS CARD: what a client holds with the shop, drawn as the card in their wallet. One per page, and the page's one orange slab (§1 rule 5).

|              |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector     | `ui-pass-card` (static, `role="group"`). There is no `button` form and no `strip` variant: the glance does not draw a pass                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Inputs       | `uiFigure: string` (required); `uiUnit: string \| null = null`; `uiHolder: string \| null = null`; `uiDetail: string \| null = null` (the quiet line under the holder); `uiState: string \| null = null` (the state line, only when it says something); `uiFrozen = false` (`booleanAttribute`); `uiLabel: string` (required, the accessible name); `uiTestId: string \| null = null` (stamps `<id>-figure`, `-holder`, `-detail`, `-state` on the parts) |
| Slots        | `[uiPassMark]` (top-leading: `ui-logo`), `[uiPassBadge]` (top-trailing: ONE plate, the tier, a projected `span[uiBadge uiOnMedia]`: the pattern imports no control)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Anatomy      | two parts inside ONE radius (`--control-radius-prominent`, `overflow: clip`): the FIELD (the upper 62 %) and the FOOT (the lower 38 %). `min-block-size` from `aspect-ratio: 1.586` (355 × 224 at 390: field 139, foot 85); a second line of the holder grows the foot, never shrinks the field                                                                                                                                                           |
| The field    | surface `var(--ui-pass-card-surface)` = `var(--sys-color-accent)`, BOTH themes; padding `comfortable`. Top row: the mark, the plate. Bottom row: the figure `largeTitle` 36/800 and the unit `title3` at weight 700, on one baseline, both `var(--ui-pass-card-ink)` = `var(--sys-color-on-accent)`. ONLY display sizes are set on the field (§1 rule 4)                                                                                                  |
| The foot     | surface `var(--ui-pass-card-foot, var(--sys-color-surface-elevated))` (white in light, the lighter surface in dark); padding `regular` block, `comfortable` inline. Leading: the holder `callout` 14/600 primary over the detail `footnote` 12 secondary. Trailing, on the holder's line: the state `footnote` 12 at 500, primary. Ordinary card text: ≥ 4.5:1 in both themes                                                                             |
| Motif        | two `aria-hidden` discs (220 and 140) in the FIELD's trailing 40 %, `var(--ui-pass-card-motif)` = `color-mix(in srgb, var(--sys-color-on-accent) 10%, transparent)`, clipped by the field. No gradient. The figure's column is the leading 60 %: no white text ever lies over the motif, so its contrast is the solid's                                                                                                                                   |
| Lift         | `--sys-elevation-raised` in light                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| States       | rest; `uiFrozen`: `filter: saturate(0.35)` on the FIELD, nothing else changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Motion       | none. The figure does not count up                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| A11y         | one name (`uiLabel`); the figure, the holder and the plate are `aria-hidden` inside it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Custom props | `--ui-pass-card-surface`, `--ui-pass-card-ink`, `--ui-pass-card-foot`, `--ui-pass-card-motif`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Showcase     | **NEW** `controls/pass-card/`: the pass, with a state line, frozen, a two-line holder, owing points, at 330, both themes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### 3.6 Ink-only changes, the `primary` style, and the metric's third rung

No API changes but the two named; the rules are §1.3's. What a consumer must know:

| Piece                                       | The change a consumer sees                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ui-date-tile uiTone="upcoming"`            | the same wash, the day in primary ink, the caption in the on-fill secondary                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `span[uiBadge uiTone="accent"]`             | the same wash, primary-ink words; with `uiIcon` the glyph is the accent; `uiProminence="increased"` is a PRIMARY fill; `uiOnMedia` is one near-black ink for words and glyph                                                                                                               |
| `button[uiButton] uiButtonStyle="bordered"` | the same wash, a primary-ink label, an accent glyph beside a label, a primary glyph when icon-only                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `button[uiChip] uiTint="accent"` selected   | an accent wash with a primary-ink label                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ui-toast`'s action                         | primary ink at weight 600                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `uiForegroundStyle="accent"` / `"promo"`    | the main accent; legal on `ui-icon` and display figures only (the guard, §1.5)                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `uiForegroundStyle="primary"`               | **CHANGED**: written explicitly it stamps `data-foreground-style="primary"` and sets the primary ink over an inherited one (≙ `.foregroundStyle(.primary)`). Left unset the directive writes nothing, as today                                                                             |
| `ui-metric`                                 | **NEW** rung `uiControlSize="extraLarge"`: the value `largeTitle` 36/800, the unit `title3` 20/600 secondary, the caption `callout` 14. `regular` (20/600) and `large` (28/700) are unchanged (`metric.css:40, 88-96`). Used by the medal detail's progress and nowhere else in this round |

### 3.7 `ui-gauge`: the circular style and the ink

|                       |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector              | `ui-gauge` (unchanged); `role="meter"`, never `progressbar`; it never performs on mount                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Inputs                | `uiValue`, `uiMin`, `uiMax`, `uiMarks`, `uiColumns`, `uiRewardIcon`, `uiLabel`, `uiValueText` (existing). `uiGaugeStyle`: `'linearCapacity' \| 'discreteCapacity' \| 'steps'` + **NEW** `'circularCapacity'` (≙ `.gaugeStyle(.accessoryCircularCapacity)`). `uiControlSize`: `'small' \| 'regular'` + **NEW** `'large' \| 'extraLarge'` (circular only)                                                                                                                                                        |
| Slots                 | **NEW** `[uiCurrentValueLabel]` (≙ SwiftUI's `currentValueLabel`): what stands in a ring's centre, `aria-hidden`. It is ONE number (or one check), always `headline` 18/600 tabular: «3», «7», «18», never «3/10»                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
