| Circular sizes (DV-H) | the size names the DISC the ring wraps; the outer sizes are R2-G's, the stroke is heavier than the first issue's: `regular` 44 → outer 52, **stroke 3, gap 1** · `large` 52 → outer 64, **stroke 4, gap 2** · `extraLarge` 112 → outer 128, **stroke 6, gap 2** (5.8 %, 6.3 % and 4.7 % of the diameter). A ring that stands ALONE around a figure (status, the collection) takes `large` with `--ui-gauge-stroke: calc(var(--sys-space-unit) * 1.25)` (5: 7.8 %, the family of `/account`'s ring, 5.25 on 72) |
| Circular drawing      | an inline `<svg>` of two circles: the track `var(--ui-gauge-track-ring, var(--sys-color-separator))`, the arc `var(--ui-gauge-tint)`, from 12 o'clock, clockwise, round caps, `stroke-dasharray` from `--ui-gauge-value`. A zero value draws no arc (no dot at 12 o'clock). `data-complete` at 1. **NEW** `data-extent="dashed"` (set by `ui-medal` for a not-started family): the track alone, dashed (2 on, 3 off), in `--ui-gauge-rim`                                                                      |
| Ink                   | `--ui-gauge-ink: var(--sys-color-foreground)` (§1.3): the current node's numeral is primary ink on the wash inside its accent ring                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Motion                | none on mount; a live change after the first frame glides the arc (`--sys-motion-duration-deliberate`); reduced motion draws the value                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| A11y                  | unchanged: `uiLabel` and `uiValueText` are required; inside a button or an image the gauge is presentational and the container's name carries the words                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Custom props          | `--ui-gauge-tint`, `--ui-gauge-ink`, `--ui-gauge-track`, `--ui-gauge-rim` (existing); NEW `--ui-gauge-stroke`, `--ui-gauge-track-ring`, `--ui-gauge-diameter` (read-only, published for a composer)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| NOT in this round     | a vertical `steps` axis and the marks' `aside` / `asideSrc` (the first issue's): the detail's steps are gone (DV-B) and its ladder is a list of medals, so `UiMark` and `mark-scale.ts` are not touched                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Not reused            | `ui-progress-ring` (`libs/ui/controls/src/lib/progress-ring/`): a control (a pattern cannot import it) that draws itself on mount. `/account` keeps it; the client stack uses the gauge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Showcase              | `controls/gauge/`: the three ring sizes round a disc, the standalone ring with a figure, dashed, complete, and the standalone ring BESIDE `/account`'s `ui-progress-ring` at 72 (the owner's reference)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### 3.8 `ui-medal`: the ring, the sizes, four states (R2-G)

|                          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selectors                | `ui-medal` (`role="img"`), `button[uiMedal]` (unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Inputs                   | `uiIcon`, `uiSrc`, `uiMonogram`, `uiLevel`, `uiTitle`, `uiCaption`, `uiValue`, `uiValueText`, `uiStacked`, `uiLabel` (existing). `uiControlSize`: `'regular' \| 'large'` + **NEW** `'extraLarge'`. `uiState`: `'notStarted' \| 'inProgress' \| 'earned'` + **NEW** `'complete'` |
| Slots                    | `[uiMedalMarker]` (existing: «Ново», top-trailing, above the ring)                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Template                 | `.ui-medal__ring` (NEW: `ui-gauge uiGaugeStyle="circularCapacity" [uiControlSize]`, `aria-hidden`) wraps `.ui-medal__disc`. `.ui-medal__meter` and its reserved line are REMOVED                                                                                                |
| Sizes                    | `regular`: disc 44, ring 52, stroke 3 · `large`: disc 52, ring 64, stroke 4 · `extraLarge`: disc 112 (`--control-size-extraLarge`), ring 128, stroke 6. Rim chip 16 / 18 / 28 tall (`caption` 11/600, 11/600, `subheadline` 13/700)                                             |
| The four states, by form | see the table below                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| The numeral rule         | unchanged: the chip is the highest EARNED level; a target lives only in words, in `uiCaption`. The chip sits at 6 o'clock over the ring, cut out of it by a 2 pt ring of the ground                                                                                             |
| One baseline             | a tile (`data-tile`) reserves the chip's room in every state, so a row of mixed states sets every title on one line; captions align per row (as built)                                                                                                                          |
| Surface                  | a ring sits on a white or elevated card, on the sheet's surface, or on the stage's white spotlight. On a grey tile or a grey segment the accent arc is 2.84:1: the medal warns in development when its nearest `ui-card` is `muted` or when it stands inside a `.ui-list-group` |
| A11y                     | one sentence names it (`uiLabel`); the ring is presentational                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Custom props             | `--ui-medal-tint`, `--ui-medal-disc`, `--ui-medal-stack-ring` (existing); `--ui-medal-meter` REMOVED                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Showcase                 | `controls/medal/`: the four states at the three sizes, a grid of mixed states, a ladder of five, the stack, art, monogram. An `earned` medal at each size stands beside the standalone ring, so the arc is judged against a filled disc                                         |

| State        | Ring                                            | Face                                                                                                               | Rim chip                                  | Caption (the consumer's)          |
| ------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| `notStarted` | the track alone, DASHED, secondary ink          | `--sys-color-fill`; the real emblem, monogram or art as a grey silhouette (art greyscale at 45 %). Never a padlock | none                                      | «0/3 до I»                        |
| `inProgress` | solid track + accent arc                        | `--sys-color-fill`; glyph primary; art stays grey (full colour means earned)                                       | none                                      | «2/3 до I»                        |
| `earned`     | solid track + accent arc towards the NEXT level | filled accent, glyph on-accent; art in full colour                                                                 | the highest earned level («II»)           | «23/25 до III»                    |
| `complete`   | a FULL accent ring                              | as earned                                                                                                          | the top numeral («V»); none for a one-off | «Всички нива»; a one-off its date |

The ring's value is progress from the last earned threshold to the next: «Фейд» at 23 with II = 10 and III = 25 draws (23 − 10) / (25 − 10) = 87 %.

### 3.9 Category chips

No new DS piece, and nothing pinned (DV-A): `button[uiChip] uiControlSize="small" [uiSelected]` (`libs/ui/controls/src/lib/chip/chip.ts`), untinted (the selected chip is the primary fill with on-primary ink, as today). They are TOGGLES that filter, which is what a chip is; a segmented control is a view toggle (house ruling) and the month pills navigate (§3.11). The band is the feature piece `lib-achievement-chips` (NEW, `libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/achievement-chips/`): inputs `chips: readonly AchievementChipVm[]`, `selectedId: string`; output `picked: string`; `ui-flow role="toolbar"`, wrapping, in flow. Its host takes `scroll-margin-block-start: var(--modal-sheet-clear-top)`, the sheet's own clearance, so a page opened on a chip lands with the band under the bar and clear of its fade. It declares no colour, no `position` and no cover. The ledger page's kind filter uses the same chips in a `ui-flow`.

### 3.10 `lib-visit-card` (NEW, shared by `/account` and the glance)

|          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector | `lib-visit-card`; folder `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/visit-card/visit-card.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}`; the pure builders in `visit-card.presenter.ts`, the types in `visit-card.vm.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Inputs   | `vm: VisitCardVm` (required); `headerProminence: 'standard' \| 'increased' = 'standard'` (≙ `.headerProminence`); `tone: UiCardTone = 'elevated'`; `padding: UiCardPadding = 'comfortable'`; `interactive = false` (the whole card a `button[uiCard]`); `showServices = true`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Outputs  | `open: void` (interactive only); `openPhoto: string`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Anatomy  | label · THE SERVICES · the facts (+ the figure at their trailing edge) · quote · thumbs · capsule; the barber's face (`ui-avatar regular`, 44) at the top-trailing corner. ONE order for every kind: a last visit and a next visit read the same way. `standard` (the glance, under a module heading): label `subheadline` 13 secondary, services `headline` 18/600, facts `callout` 14 secondary with ONE fact in primary/500 (the date of a last visit, the time of a next one). `increased` (`/account`, no heading above): label `headline` 18/600, facts `body` 15 primary, and with `showServices` false no services line: exactly today's «Предстоящ час» card |
| Capsule  | a status that is news: «Потвърден» / «Заявен» on a next visit (`appointments.status.*`); none on a last visit or a waiting request                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| States   | the host's: the card draws a VM or nothing; loading, empty and error stay with `/account` and with the glance's module                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| A11y     | static: `role="group"` with `vm.accessibleName`; interactive: the button's name. Thumbs are buttons of their own and sit OUTSIDE an interactive card's button                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Test ids | the host's, through `vm.testId` (`client-visit-last`, `client-visit-next`, `client-visit-waitlist`, `account-upcoming`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

```ts
export type VisitCardKind = 'last' | 'next' | 'waitlist';
/** One run of a quiet line; `fact` runs are set in primary ink at weight 500. */
export interface VisitLinePart {
  readonly text: string;
  readonly fact: boolean;
}
export interface VisitCardVm {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: VisitCardKind;
  /** «Последно посещение» · «Следващ час» · «Предстоящ час» · «Чака свободен час». */
  readonly label: string;
  /** Always the services: «Фейд · Оформяне на брада». */
  readonly services: string;
  /** last: «5.09 · Иван · Център» · next: «пт, 23.10 · 10:00 · Иван» · waitlist: «8.10 – 9.10, следобед · още 1». */
  readonly facts: readonly VisitLinePart[];
  /** «27,50 €» (`formatMoney`): staff, a worked visit. */
  readonly figure: string | null;
  readonly quote: string | null;
  readonly capsule: {
    readonly text: string;
    readonly tone: UiBadgeTone;
  } | null;
  readonly barber: {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly avatarSrc: string | null;
  } | null;
  readonly photos: readonly VisitRowPhoto[];
  readonly accessibleName: string;
  readonly testId: string;
}
export function visitCardOf(
  appointment: Appointment,
  options: VisitCardOptions,
  lookup: VisitLookup,
): VisitCardVm;
export function waitlistCardOf(
  request: WaitlistRequest,
  options: WaitlistCardOptions,
  lookup: VisitLookup,
): VisitCardVm;
```

`/account` mounts it with `headerProminence="increased"`, `interactive`, `[showServices]="false"`, no barber, and looks exactly as it does today (its four test ids kept). Whether it should gain the service and the barber is owner question Q7: then it is `showServices` and nothing else.

### 3.11 `lib-visits-browser`, `lib-visit-pills`, the row's changes (R2-I), and the rule's words

|                   |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Selector          | `lib-visits-browser` (NEW), presentational, no store injected; folder `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/visits-browser/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Inputs            | `sections: readonly VisitSectionVm[]` (required); `perspective: VisitPerspective` (required); `listStyle: VisitListStyle = 'insetGrouped'`; `state: VisitListState = 'ready'`; `paging: VisitListPaging \| null = null`; `testIdPrefix: string \| null = null`; `pills = true`; `bleed: 'viewport' \| 'container' = 'viewport'`; `interactive: boolean \| null = null` (`null` = the perspective's own: the client's rows are buttons, the staff's static; Q6 sets it)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Outputs           | `openRow: string`; `loadMore: void`; `retry: void`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Slots             | `[visitListEmpty]` (the host words its own empty); **`[visitPillsAction]`** (ONE control pinned at the pill rail's trailing end: the staff page's funnel)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Owns              | the scroll-spy and `jumpTo`, moved from `client-appointments.ts:275-280, 688-719, 814-823`. The observer's root is `nearestScroller(host)` (§3.3; `null` = the viewport). Its top margin is a LENGTH IN PX (an `IntersectionObserver` takes px or %, never a custom property, and `getPropertyValue` of a custom property returns the unresolved `calc(…)` string): the browser reads the COMPUTED `scroll-margin-block-start` of its first section anchor, a real property that resolves to px, and builds `rootMargin: '-<n>px 0px -70% 0px'` from it. The anchors take `scroll-margin-block-start: var(--visit-list-anchor-clearance)`, which the HOST states: the client page `6rem` (today's literal 96, `client-appointments.ts:706`); the staff page `calc(var(--modal-sheet-chrome-height) + var(--sys-space-compact))` in `client-history.css`. It re-derives the pills from `sections()` and resets the current pill when they change (a filter) |
| `lib-visit-pills` | the jump capsules, moved from `.appointments__pill*` (`client-appointments.css:155-181`): `button`, 36 tall, `subheadline` 600, the control fill, the current one inverted (primary fill). The move ADDS what the original lacks: the house state layer (`@media (hover: hover)` … `::after { background: var(--sys-state-layer-hover) }`) and its ungated `:active` mirror; the spec pins both. A pill NAVIGATES (no `aria-pressed`); the rail is `nav` with `aria-label` «Месеци» and `aria-current="true"` on the current pill. Inputs `sections`, `activeKey`, `bleed`, `testIdPrefix`; output `jump: string`. The band is a grid `[rail 1fr] [action auto]`: the rail is `ui-scroll-row uiSpacing="compact" [uiFullBleed]` (`true` for the viewport, `'container'` in a sheet) and fades under its trailing edge; the action column holds the projected `[visitPillsAction]` and is absent without one                                                |
| Test ids          | `{prefix}-pills`, `{prefix}-pill` (+ `data-section`, `data-selected`), and the list's own; with `appointments` they are exactly today's                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Calendar          | not in this round: the client page keeps its own calendar view and toggle                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

**`lib-visit-row` (CHANGED).** In the `staff` perspective the row ends in the barber's face, in a 48 column that is RESERVED on every row (§2.5), and carries the staff's fact at the end of its second line:

```ts
/** What ends a staff row's second line: money, a state in words, or «Този час». At most one. */
export type VisitTrailingFact =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'amount';
      readonly text: string;
      readonly emphasis: 'primary' | 'secondary';
      readonly struck: boolean;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'status';
      readonly icon: UiIconName;
      readonly text: string;
      readonly tone: 'neutral' | 'warning';
    }
  | { readonly kind: 'badge'; readonly text: string }
  | null;

export interface VisitRowVm {
  // …every field it has
  /** Staff only; null on the client's row. NEW in W0. */
  readonly trailingFact: VisitTrailingFact;
}
```

`VisitRowVm.trailing` keeps its TYPE (the union) through the round and V2's presenter fills it with the barber for both perspectives; it narrows to `Extract<VisitTrailing, { kind: 'barber' }> | null` in W4. `variant`, `stampSize` and `VisitRowVm.photos` are **deprecated** in W1 (they keep working: the built glance mounts the card variant until W2) and removed in W4. `VisitGrain` keeps `'year'` (the type and `visits.tile.month.*` stay for a future dense list); no page of this round uses it. `listStyle="plain"` stays in `lib-visit-list` for the same reason.

**`rhythmRuleWords`** (NEW, `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/rhythm-rule/rhythm-rule.words.ts`): the rule's sentences are COPY, and copy is a feature's (the precedent: `visitWordsOf`, in this same lib). ONE pure function words the rule for the client card and for the admin page, and neither feature imports the other:

```ts
export interface RhythmRuleWords {
  /** «Посещение на всеки 6 седмици пази ритъма.» */
  readonly sentence: string;
  /** «7 дни гратис след срока.» · «До 2 защити …» · … in the rule's own order. */
  readonly details: readonly string[];
  /** «На всеки 6 седмици · 7 дни гратис» */
  readonly short: string;
  readonly days: (count: number) => string;
  readonly weeks: (count: number) => string;
}
export function rhythmRuleWords(
  summary: StreakRuleSummary,
  translate: VisitTranslate,
  lang: string,
): RhythmRuleWords;
```

`StreakRuleSummary` is the domain's structure (§4.1), taken from the application barrel.

---

## 4. Data and domain

House style throughout (memory: rich domain models): private constructors, `static create(props)` → `Result<T, E[]>`, class-based errors in `*.errors.ts`, mappers that go through the factories, ports defined by the application layer and implemented by adapters registered in the LAZY module (`libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/firestore.adapters.ts`). Projections stay flat readonly shapes (the first spec's rule).

### 4.1 The rhythm as a managed rule (R2-J)

NEW files in `libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/loyalty/`: `streak-interval.ts`, `streak-qualifier.ts`, `streak-definition.ts`, `streak-definition.errors.ts`, `streak-schedule.ts`, `streak-projector.ts`, each with its spec. `cadence.kind: 'calendar'` and `freezes.autoApply` leave the model (rhythm-admin §3.3).

**The bridge (W1 → W4).** The tree must build at the end of every wave (§7.0), and the built wallet reads the flat interface until W2. So in W1 the flat `StreakDefinition` of `catalog-entries.ts:95-117` is RENAMED `StreakDefinitionEntry` (its shape untouched, marked `@deprecated`), the class below takes the name `StreakDefinition`, and `STREAK_EXCUSES` / `StreakExcuse` move to `streak-definition.ts` and gain `closedDays` (`catalog-entries.ts` re-exports them). `LoyaltyCatalog` keeps `streaks: readonly StreakDefinitionEntry[]` beside the new `streakSchedules`; the mapper derives each entry from the rule in force (`streakEntryOf`, §4.3), so the built page reads the PUBLISHED rule through its old shape. The built presenter's one import (`loyalty.presenter.ts:7`) becomes `type StreakDefinitionEntry as StreakDefinition`: one line, R2b's in W1 (the package that adds the name to the application barrel). In W4 the entry, the field, the deriving function and that import are deleted.

```ts
// streak-interval.ts
export type StreakIntervalProps =
  | { kind: 'fixed'; days: number }
  | {
      kind: 'personal';
      tolerancePct: number;
      minDays: number;
      maxDays: number;
      fallbackDays: number;
    };
export class StreakInterval {
  static create(
    props: StreakIntervalProps,
  ): Result<StreakInterval, StreakDefinitionError[]>;
  /** The days of the period that opens now; `habitDays` = the mean of the last six counted gaps, or null. */
  daysFor(habitDays: number | null): {
    readonly days: number;
    readonly source: 'fixed' | 'personal' | 'fallback';
  };
  get shortestDays(): number;
}

// streak-qualifier.ts
export type StreakQualifierProps =
  | { kind: 'anyVisit' }
  | { kind: 'services'; serviceIds: readonly string[] }
  | { kind: 'categories'; categoryIds: readonly string[] };
export class StreakQualifier {
  static create(
    props: StreakQualifierProps,
  ): Result<StreakQualifier, StreakDefinitionError[]>;
  counts(visit: {
    readonly serviceIds: readonly ServiceId[];
    readonly categoryIds: readonly ServiceCategoryId[];
    readonly upsellOnlyIds: readonly ServiceId[];
  }): boolean;
}

// streak-definition.ts
export const STREAK_EXCUSES = [
  'shopClosure',
  'barberAbsence',
  'staffCancelled',
  'closedDays',
] as const;
export type StreakExcuse = (typeof STREAK_EXCUSES)[number];
export interface StreakDefinitionProps {
  id: string;
  name: LocalizedTextProps;
  version: number;
  effectiveFrom: IsoDate;
  enabled: boolean; // P1
  qualifier: StreakQualifierProps; // P2
  minGapDays: number; // P3   0–14, < interval
  bookingHold: { enabled: boolean; maxDaysPastDue: number }; // P4   0–28
  interval: StreakIntervalProps; // P5   14–182 days
  graceDays: number;
  riskLeadDays: number; // P6, P7   0–21, < interval
  protections: {
    enabled: boolean;
    maxBanked: number;
    earnEvery: number;
    startWith: number;
  }; // P8
  excuses: readonly StreakExcuse[]; // P9
  clientPause: { enabled: boolean; maxDaysPerYear: number }; // P10  0–180
  repair: { enabled: boolean; windowDays: number; perYear: number }; // P11  1–60, 1–4
  onBreak: 'restart' | 'hold'; // P12
}
export class StreakDefinition {
  static create(
    props: StreakDefinitionProps,
  ): Result<StreakDefinition, StreakDefinitionError[]>;
  static default(): StreakDefinition; // fixed 42 days, grace 7, lead 7, 2 protections every 5, the three shop excuses, repair 14 / 1, restart
  summary(): StreakRuleSummary; // structure, never copy: the domain owns no strings
  changeFrom(previous: StreakDefinition): StreakRuleChange;
  revise(
    patch: Partial<StreakDefinitionProps>,
    effectiveFrom: IsoDate,
  ): Result<StreakDefinition, StreakDefinitionError[]>;
  equals(other: StreakDefinition): boolean;
  toProps(): StreakDefinitionProps;
}
export interface StreakRuleSummary {
  readonly enabled: boolean;
  readonly interval: StreakIntervalProps;
  readonly graceDays: number;
  readonly protections: {
    readonly maxBanked: number;
    readonly earnEvery: number;
  } | null;
  readonly shopExcuses: boolean;
  readonly hold: { readonly maxDaysPastDue: number } | null;
  readonly repair: { readonly windowDays: number } | null;
  readonly holdsCount: boolean;
  readonly qualifier: StreakQualifierProps;
}
export type StreakRuleDirection =
  'looser' | 'stricter' | 'mixed' | 'neutral' | 'switch';
export interface StreakRuleChange {
  readonly direction: StreakRuleDirection;
  readonly changed: readonly (keyof StreakDefinitionProps)[];
}

// streak-schedule.ts: the head document as a model
export type StreakTransition = 'grandfather' | 'recompute';
export class StreakDefinitionSchedule {
  static create(props: {
    current: StreakDefinition;
    scheduled: {
      definition: StreakDefinition;
      transition: StreakTransition;
    } | null;
  }): Result<StreakDefinitionSchedule, StreakDefinitionError[]>; // scheduledNotAfterCurrent · versionNotRising · idMismatch
  inForceOn(today: IsoDate): StreakDefinition;
  upcomingOn(
    today: IsoDate,
  ): { definition: StreakDefinition; transition: StreakTransition } | null;
  static earliestEffectiveDay(
    change: StreakRuleChange,
    today: IsoDate,
    policy: { clientsSeeRhythm: boolean; noticeDays: number },
  ): IsoDate;
}
```

Errors (`streak-definition.errors.ts`, one class each, extending `StreakDefinitionError` with `code`, `field`, `value`): `StreakIntervalOutOfRangeError`, `StreakToleranceOutOfRangeError`, `StreakClampInvertedError`, `StreakFallbackOutsideClampError`, `StreakGraceOutOfRangeError`, `StreakGraceNotShorterThanIntervalError`, `StreakRiskLeadOutOfRangeError`, `StreakMinGapOutOfRangeError`, `StreakProtectionsOutOfRangeError`, `StreakStartWithAboveCapError`, `StreakRepairOutOfRangeError`, `StreakBookingHoldOutOfRangeError`, `StreakClientPauseOutOfRangeError`, `StreakQualifierEmptyError`, `StreakUnknownExcuseError`, `StreakEffectiveDayInvalidError`, `StreakVersionNotPositiveError`.

`LoyaltyCatalog` (`loyalty-catalog.ts:46`) gains `streakSchedules: readonly StreakDefinitionSchedule[]` and `streakInForce(id: string, today: IsoDate): StreakDefinition | null`; `LoyaltyCatalogParts` gains `streakSchedules` (optional, `[]` by default, so every fixture and fake that builds a catalogue today still compiles).

The report's ten owner questions take its defaults (R2-J): ship both interval kinds, start on fixed 6 weeks; a booking holds the due date up to 14 days; any worked visit counts; a miss restarts with the earn-back; the shop's excuses may be switched off with the consequence stated; a stricter rule from tomorrow while no client sees the rhythm; `admin` and `sysadmin` manage it; one rule for the business; a callable; no interval by service.

### 4.2 `projectStreak`, and what the projection gains

```ts
// streak-projector.ts: the seed's `rhythmOf` (tools/seed/loyalty.mjs:412-557), moved here and typed
export interface StreakFacts {
  readonly visits: readonly {
    readonly day: IsoDate;
    readonly counted: boolean;
  }[]; // the qualifier already applied
  readonly excused: readonly {
    readonly day: IsoDate;
    readonly reason: StreakExcuse;
  }[];
  readonly bookings: readonly {
    readonly placedOn: IsoDate;
    readonly day: IsoDate;
  }[];
  readonly pauses: readonly {
    readonly from: IsoDate;
    readonly until: IsoDate;
    readonly reason: 'pause' | 'clientPause';
  }[];
  readonly grants: readonly { readonly day: IsoDate; readonly count: number }[];
}
export function projectStreak(
  definition: StreakDefinition,
  facts: StreakFacts,
  today: IsoDate,
): StreakState | null;
```

`projections.ts` (CHANGED, flat as before):

```ts
export interface StreakPeriodFact {
  readonly mark: 'kept' | 'protected' | 'repaired' | 'missed';
  readonly on: IsoDate;
  readonly reason: 'freeze' | StreakExcuse | 'pause' | 'clientPause' | null;
}
export interface StreakState {
  // …the eight fields it has
  readonly period: {
    readonly intervalDays: number;
    readonly graceDays: number;
    readonly source: 'fixed' | 'personal' | 'fallback';
    readonly habitDays: number | null;
  } | null;
  readonly periods: readonly StreakPeriodFact[]; // the newest twelve closed periods, oldest first
  readonly heldBy: {
    readonly appointmentId: string;
    readonly on: IsoDate;
  } | null;
}
// StreakPhase's `frozen.reason` gains 'excuse'
```

`StreakState`'s three new fields are REQUIRED; the mapper fills them (`null`, `[]`, `null` for a document written before this round), so nothing that reads a state has to guess.

**The card stops holding a second copy of the rule.** The NEW wallet presenter (`wallet.presenter.ts`, §7.3) maps `StreakState.periods` and appends the open mark from `phase`, `dueBy`, `heldBy`; its in-chair due date reads `state.period.intervalDays`; it has no fallback to a streak without a definition. The built `rhythmPeriods` (`loyalty.presenter.ts:246`), `inChairDueOf` (`:353`) and `defaultStreakOf` (`:184`), which re-derive the periods from the visits, are not rewritten: they serve the built page until W2 and are deleted in W4. Visibility:

```
showRhythm = flags.isEnabled('loyalty.streaks')
          && catalog.streakInForce(streakId, today)?.enabled === true
          && account.streaks[streakId] !== undefined
```

When false, all of these are absent, heading included: the glance's rhythm fact, the wallet's «Ритъм», the families `series-rhythm` and `series-comeback` unless already earned, the records «най-дълъг ритъм» and «Ритъм сега».

Specs: a table-driven `streak-definition.spec.ts` (every error, every boundary); `changeFrom` direction cases; `streak-projector.spec.ts` that PORTS the seed's expectations (Георги, Мартин with his shop cancel) and adds one case per parameter; three property tests (lead ruling G1-8; the single property first written here, "loosening any single parameter never lowers `current`", is false for the rule as a whole: a protection is earned by the run's count, the reserve has a cap and the earn-back is rationed by the year, so up to 0.8 % of random histories end lower under a looser rule): (a) on the plain clock (protections off, earn-back off) no clock loosening lowers `current` or `best`; (b) under any rule `restart → hold` never does; (c) under any rule the FIRST day two counts differ favours the looser rule (all loosenings but `protections.earnEvery`). Three counter-examples are pinned as cases. `changeFrom`'s `looser` is therefore a statement about the rule, never a promise per client; the admin preview COUNTS both projections.

### 4.3 Wire documents and rules

`streakDefinitions/{streakId}` (the head: `name`, `current`, `scheduled | null`, `updatedAt`) and `streakDefinitions/{streakId}/versions/{version:06}` (immutable: the body plus `publishedAt`, `publishedBy`, `transition`, `direction`, `changed`, `requestId`, `withdrawnAt`). `effectiveFrom` is a Sofia DAY. A reader resolves the rule in force BY DATE (`inForceOn(today)`), so no job is needed to flip it.

`libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/loyalty-document.ts` (CHANGED): NEW `StreakDefinitionHeadDocument` (the head shape) and `StreakDefinitionVersionDocument`; NEW `streakScheduleFromDocument` returns a `StreakDefinitionSchedule` through the factories, and it is TOLERANT for this round: a document in the flat shape the emulator holds before the seed runs again is read as `current` with `scheduled: null`. NEW `streakDefinitionToDocument` beside it (the `booking-policy-document.ts` precedent). NEW `streakEntryOf(definition: StreakDefinition): StreakDefinitionEntry` (the bridge of §4.1: `cadence` = `rollingDays` of `interval.daysFor(null).days`, `freezes` from `protections` with `autoApply: true`, `excused` from `excuses`); the built `streakDefinitionFromDocument` keeps its name and return type and is re-based on the two (`streakEntryOf(schedule.inForceOn(today))`). `StreakStateDocument` gains `period`, `periods`, `heldBy` (all optional on the wire). The fixtures `libs/application/engagement/src/testing/fixtures/loyalty/catalog.json` and the account fixtures follow, and the in-memory fakes of `libs/application/engagement/src/testing.ts`.

`firestore.rules` (CHANGED at `:338-341`):

```
match /streakDefinitions/{streakId} {
  allow read: if isSignedIn();          // staff now; clients with loyalty.selfView
  allow write: if false;                // published by the callable
  match /versions/{version} {
    allow read: if isAdmin();           // the history is the admin page's
    allow write: if false;
  }
}
```

`libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/firestore.rules.integration.spec.ts` gains: a barber reads the head and is refused a version; an admin reads a version; an admin's CLIENT write is refused; an anonymous read is refused. No new index (a subcollection ordered by document id).

### 4.4 The callable, the port, the use cases, the preview

**`publishStreakDefinition`** (NEW, `apps/functions/src/lib/loyalty/publish-streak-definition.ts`, exported from `apps/functions/src/main.ts`):

```ts
interface PublishStreakDefinitionRequest {
  readonly requestId: string; // a UUID per intent, kept across retries
  readonly streakId: string;
  readonly baseVersion: number; // optimistic: refuse when the head moved
  readonly draft: StreakDefinitionBodyDocument;
  readonly effectiveFrom: string; // a Sofia day
  readonly transition: 'grandfather' | 'recompute';
  readonly previewHash: string | null; // required once the server preview exists
}
type PublishStreakDefinitionResult =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'published';
      readonly version: number;
      readonly effectiveFrom: string;
      readonly direction: StreakRuleDirection;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'refused';
      readonly code:
        | 'notAdmin'
        | 'invalid'
        | 'stale'
        | 'tooEarly'
        | 'recomputeNeedsEngine'
        | 'previewStale';
      readonly fields: readonly string[];
    };
```

It follows the house shape of every callable: the file above is the THIN callable (it reads the request and the caller), the decision is `apps/functions/src/use-cases/publish-streak-definition.use-case.ts` (+ `publish-streak-definition.errors.ts`, + spec), the writes are `apps/functions/src/adapters/firestore-streak-definition-store.ts`. The caller is `admin | sysadmin` by the ONE gate the app has: the VERIFIED token's claims, through NEW `callerIsAdmin(request)` beside `callerWorksTheBook` in `apps/functions/src/lib/booking/caller-roles.ts:13-38` (the same source `firestore.rules:25-29` reads; no second read of `users/{uid}`). The use case builds the draft through `StreakDefinition.create`, classifies the change, checks the effective day, and the adapter writes the version, the head and ONE `auditLog` row in one transaction (the `apps/functions/src/lib/booking/audit.ts` precedent; ids and codes only): `action: 'loyalty.streak_definition_published'` (or `_withdrawn`), `resourceId: 'streakDefinitions/cadence'`, `context: { from, to, effectiveFrom, transition, direction, changed }`, `changeKey: requestId`. A retry with the same `requestId` is a no-op. It contains NO engine: `transition: 'recompute'` is refused.

**The port** (NEW, `libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/streak-definition.port.ts`; a new token, because the catalogue reader's fakes live in several libs):

```ts
export interface StreakDefinitionRepository {
  observe(
    streakId: string,
  ): Observable<Result<StreakDefinitionSchedule, RepositoryError>>;
  listVersions(
    streakId: string,
    limit: number,
  ): Promise<Result<readonly StreakDefinitionVersion[], RepositoryError>>;
  publish(
    request: PublishStreakDefinition,
  ): Promise<Result<PublishedStreakDefinition, PublishStreakDefinitionError>>;
  withdraw(
    streakId: string,
    version: number,
    requestId: string,
  ): Promise<Result<void, PublishStreakDefinitionError>>;
}
export const STREAK_DEFINITION_REPOSITORY =
  new InjectionToken<StreakDefinitionRepository>('StreakDefinitionRepository');
```

Adapter `FirestoreStreakDefinitionRepository` (NEW, `libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/streak-definition-repository.adapter.ts`), bound with `provideLazyPort` in `apps/web/src/app/app.config.ts`. `FirestoreLoyaltyCatalogReader` keeps its session memo and gains `invalidate()`, called after a publish in the same session; other open sessions see the new rule on reload (the rule takes effect on a later day anyway). An in-memory fake joins `@creativo/application/engagement/testing`.

**Use cases** (NEW, `libs/application/engagement/src/lib/use-cases/`): `ObserveStreakDefinitionUseCase`, `PublishStreakDefinitionUseCase` (validates locally first, so the page names the field before a round trip), `PreviewStreakDefinitionUseCase` (pure: two `projectStreak` runs over a list of facts).

**The preview is an ESTIMATE, in the browser.** The page reads `clientStats` ONCE per visit to the page (the store keeps the facts; a recompute reads nothing), in pages of 200, five pages at most (past 1 000 clients the card says «от първите 1 000 клиенти»), through a NEW port `CLIENT_STATS_DIRECTORY` (`libs/application/booking/src/lib/ports/client-stats-directory.port.ts`: `page(limit: number, after: string | null)`, adapter in the same Firestore lib; the existing rule lets the book read `clientStats`) and folds each person's `last365.visitsCompleted` through the projector twice, once per rule. It is labelled «приблизително» because `clientStats` holds visit instants only (`VisitFact` is `{ appointmentId, atMs }`, `libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/client-stats.ts:26`): exact for the phase of a run under «всяко посещение», approximate for counts longer than a year, UNAVAILABLE for a list of services. The exact, hashed server preview is the engine's.

### 4.5 Gift vouchers by holder (Fresha adopt-now N1)

```ts
// NEW: libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/client-vouchers-reader.port.ts
export interface ClientVouchersReader {
  /** The vouchers issued to this account, any state; the presenter keeps `active` with a balance. */
  listForHolder(
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<Result<readonly GiftVoucher[], RepositoryError>>;
}
export const CLIENT_VOUCHERS_READER = new InjectionToken<ClientVouchersReader>(
  'ClientVouchersReader',
);
```

Adapter `FirestoreClientVouchersReader` (NEW, `client-vouchers-reader.adapter.ts`): `where('issuedToUserId', '==', uid)` (rules are not filters; the rule at `firestore.rules:506-510` already allows the book to list); documents through `gift-voucher-document.ts`'s reader. A single-field equality: no index. A NEW token, not a method on `GIFT_VOUCHER_READER` (its fakes). A paper voucher has no holder and never appears on a card: it is entered by code at the bill. A `void` voucher is absent; an empty active one is absent.

### 4.6 The coupon's occasion

`Coupon` (`libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/coupon.ts`) gains `occasion: CouponOccasion | null` (NEW; `CouponOccasion = 'birthday'`, a closed set; an unknown value is `CouponOccasionUnknownError`). In `CreateCouponProps` and in `Coupon.reconstitute`'s props it is OPTIONAL and `null` by default, so no domain or infrastructure fixture has to change. Persisted as `coupons/{id}.occasion` (`libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/coupon-persistence.ts:129`): the mapper reads a missing field as `null` and WRITES the field back on every save, so an admin's edit of the birthday coupon (`firestore.rules:496-499` lets an admin write) does not drop it; one rules-integration case pins the round trip. The server reads coupon fields ad hoc (`firestore-discount-resolver.ts:56-59`): the field is additive there. the seed's `coupon-birthday` (`tools/seed-dev-catalog.mjs:631`) carries `occasion: 'birthday'`. `VisitEditorGrantOption` (`staff-visit-editor.ts:618`) and `ClientCardGrant` gain `occasion`. The birthday card carries a grant only when the grant's coupon says so; a name is never parsed.

### 4.7 The activity read (Fresha adopt-now N2)

```ts
// projections.ts (NEW, flat)
export type ActivityEffect =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'points';
      readonly delta: number;
      readonly balanceAfter: number;
    }
  | { readonly kind: 'achievement'; readonly op: 'granted' | 'revoked' }
  | { readonly kind: 'tier'; readonly from: string; readonly to: string }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'reward';
      readonly op: 'granted' | 'reinstated' | 'revoked';
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'redemption';
      readonly op: 'redeemed' | 'reversed' | 'expired';
      readonly cost: number;
    };
export interface ActivityEntry {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly seq: number;
  readonly at: Date;
  /** Entries of one event share it: a visit's seats, a level and its bonus, a redemption and its grant. */
  readonly correlationId: string;
  readonly effect: ActivityEffect;
  readonly label: {
    readonly code: string;
    readonly params: Readonly<Record<string, string | number>>;
  };
  readonly reverses: string | null;
}
export type ActivityKind = 'all' | 'points' | 'rewards' | 'status';

// NEW: libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/loyalty-activity-reader.port.ts
export interface LoyaltyActivityReader {
  page(
    userId: string,
    query: {
      readonly kind: ActivityKind;
      readonly limit: number;
      readonly after: number | null;
    },
  ): Promise<
    Result<
      {
        readonly entries: readonly ActivityEntry[];
        readonly next: number | null;
      },
      RepositoryError
    >
  >;
}
export const LOYALTY_ACTIVITY_READER =
  new InjectionToken<LoyaltyActivityReader>('LoyaltyActivityReader');
```

- **The query.** `userId ==`, `visibility == 'client'` (the rule requires it), `effect.kind in [...]`, `orderBy('seq', 'desc')`, `startAfter(after)`. The kinds: `all` = `points`, `achievement`, `tier`, `reward`, `redemption`; `points` = `points`; `rewards` = `reward`, `redemption`; `status` = `tier`. Streak, freeze, pause and visit-outcome rows are never in the feed.
- **The index.** The EXISTING composite `clientJournal (userId ASC, visibility ASC, effect.kind ASC, seq DESC)` (`firestore.indexes.json:304`) serves every pill, «Всички» included (an `in` is a union of equalities on the same index). **No new index.** (The all-kinds index `userId, visibility, seq DESC` is needed only if streak rows ever join the feed; it is not added.)
- **The mapper.** NEW `activityEntryFromDocument` beside `ledgerEntryFromDocument` (`loyalty-document.ts:909`), reading `correlationId` and `balanceAfter`, which the document already carries (`:594`, `:601`).
- **Folding** is the presenter's (`foldByEvent`, pure): entries of one `correlationId` become one row; the amount is the sum of the point deltas; the balance after is the newest entry's; the row's kind is the highest-ranked effect (redemption, reward, tier, achievement, points). A page boundary can cut an event: the presenter holds back the last event of a page until the next page (or the end) confirms it is whole.
- `LOYALTY_READER.listLedgerTail` stays (the first spec's contract) and is no longer called by the card; it is removed when nothing else reads it.

### 4.8 Records

No new read and no new domain kind. `LoyaltyAccount.records` (`projections.ts:107`) carries the four kinds of `PERSONAL_RECORD_KINDS`; the page's five other rows are facts the store already holds (§2.3.4): «Ритъм сега» is the default streak's `current` and the day its run began (from `StreakState.periods`); «Общо посещения», «Последните 12 месеца», «Бръснар по избор» and «Обичайни услуги» are `ClientStats`'s `lifetime.visitsCompleted`, `last365.visitsCompleted`, `preferredBarberId` and `usualServiceIds` (read for the head through `CLIENT_STATS_READER`); «По години» is counted from the store's loaded history under the completeness rule of §2.3.4. «Нов рекорд» is `on` (or the year's end for `mostVisitsInYear`) within the last 30 days of the shop's today. A row's `id` is a string of the card's own (`RecordRowVm.id`), not a `PersonalRecordKind`: the domain's set does not grow. More KINDS (a favourite shop, most services in one visit) need domain work and are owner question Q9.

### 4.9 The contract (W0 publishes it; every feature package builds against it)

**New beside built, never in place.** The tree builds at the end of every wave (§7.0), and the built pages are on screen until their bodies are re-drawn in W2. So the contract of revision 3 is ADDED: a new VM file, new presenter files, new members on the store. The built VM file, the built presenters and the built store members are not edited (but for `export` keywords, §7.3) and are deleted in W4. Names that would collide take the page's new name (the head is a `hero`, the glance an `overview`, «Лоялност» a `wallet`, «Постижения» `medals`, «Посещения» `visits`); they are the FINAL names, not temporary ones.

`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-card.contracts.ts` (CHANGED, additive):

```ts
export interface ClientCardGrant {
  /* …as today… */ readonly occasion?: 'birthday' | null;
} // NEW, optional: absent ≙ null
export type LoyaltyAnchor =
  'catalog' | 'stamps' | 'rhythm' | 'wallet' | 'status'; // + 'wallet', 'status'
export type ClientCardRegion =
  | 'profile'
  | 'loyalty'
  | 'achievements'
  | 'visits'
  | 'photos'
  | 'history'
  | 'vouchers'
  | 'activity'; // + 2
```

`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-pages.vm.ts` (**NEW**). What the new pages still read of the built file (`Slice`, `SliceStatus`, `GaugeVm`, `MedalVm`, `AchievementTileVm`, `SeasonCardVm`, `EventCardVm`, `ArchiveRowVm`, `DetailRelatedVm`, `QuickActionVm`, `RewardReadyVm`, `ClientCapsulesVm`, `ClientPhotoVm`, `StampCardVm`, `HistoryCellVm`, `HistoryPagingVm`, `AchievementsData`) it imports from `client-card.vm.ts`; in W4 those move here and the built file goes.

```ts
/** One run of a quiet line; a `fact` run is set in primary ink at weight 500. */

  readonly text: string;
  readonly fact: boolean;
}

// ── the glance ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface HeroTierVm {
  readonly name: string; // «Злато»
  readonly progress: number | null; // 0 … 1 to the next tier; 1 at the top

}
export interface ClientHeroVm {

  readonly line: readonly LinePart[] | null;
  readonly tier: HeroTierVm | null;

}

  readonly kind: 'today' | 'upcoming';

  readonly gift: RewardReadyVm | null; // today only, the owner's, usable

export interface HoldingFactVm {

  readonly label: string; // «Ваучер» · «Печати» · «Ритъм»

  readonly unit: string | null; // «точки»

// ── «Лоялност» ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface PassVm {

export interface WalletActionVm {

  readonly label: string;

  readonly accessibleName: string;
  readonly grantId: string | null; // 'use' with exactly one ready ticket
  readonly count: number | null; // 'use' with more than one: the badge
}
export type WalletHoldingVm =
  | { readonly kind: 'voucher'; readonly voucher: VoucherVm }
  | { readonly kind: 'ticket'; readonly ticket: RewardReadyVm }

      readonly kind: 'stamps';

  readonly figure: string;
  readonly unit: string; // «9» · «посещения в ритъм»
  readonly phase: { readonly text: string; readonly tone: UiBadgeTone } | null; // null while healthy
  readonly periods: readonly UiStreakPeriod[];
  readonly trackName: string;
  readonly stateLine: string;
  readonly best: string | null;
  readonly protections: string | null;
  readonly ruleLine: string; // «На всеки 6 седмици · 7 дни гратис»
  readonly ruleFull: readonly string[]; // the rhythmRule.* sentences
  readonly ruleUpcoming: string | null; // «От 1.10: на всеки 5 седмици»
  readonly frozen: boolean;
}
export interface WalletTierVm {
  readonly ring: GaugeVm;
  readonly remaining: string | null; // «7»; null at the top (the ring holds a check)
  readonly line1: string; // «до Платина» · «Най-високият статус»
  readonly line2: string; // «13 посещения за 12 месеца»
  readonly steps: GaugeVm;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface RewardTileVm {
  readonly rewardId: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly cost: string;
  readonly affordable: boolean;
  readonly missing: string | null; // «още 29 т.»
  readonly progress: number | null; // balance ÷ cost, out-of-reach tiles only
  readonly src: string | null; // the cover of the ONE service its coupon names
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface ActivityRowVm {
  readonly entryId: string;
  readonly icon: UiIconName;
  readonly tone: 'accent' | 'neutral'; // the GLYPH's ink; the disc is white
  readonly title: string;
  readonly lines: readonly StaffEventLine[];
  readonly struck: boolean;
  readonly amount: string | null; // «+27 т.» · «−250 т.»; null when no points moved
  readonly balanceAfter: string | null; // «371 т.»
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface ActivityGroupVm {
  readonly key: string; // YYYY-MM
  readonly label: string; // «Септември 2026 г.»
  readonly net: string | null; // «+27 т.»; null for a month not wholly loaded
  readonly rows: readonly ActivityRowVm[];
}
export interface WalletVm {
  readonly frozen: boolean;
  readonly pass: PassVm;
  readonly actions: readonly WalletActionVm[];
  readonly holdings: Slice<readonly WalletHoldingVm[]>;
  readonly holdingsDetail: string | null; // «3» · «3 · 55,00 €»
  readonly rhythm?: WalletRhythmVm;
  readonly tier?: Slice<WalletTierVm>;
  readonly catalog?: Slice<{
    readonly name: string;
    readonly tiles: readonly RewardTileVm[];
  }>;
  readonly activity?: Slice<readonly ActivityGroupVm[]>;
}
export interface LedgerPageVm {
  readonly status: SliceStatus;
  readonly subline: readonly LinePart[];
  readonly filter: ActivityKind;
  readonly groups: readonly ActivityGroupVm[];
  readonly paging: {
    readonly kind: 'more' | 'loading' | 'failed' | 'end';
  } | null;
}

// ── «Постижения», «Рекорди», the medal ──────────────────────────────────
export interface AchievementChipVm {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface RecordCardVm {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: PersonalRecordKind;
  readonly label: string; // «Най-дълъг ритъм», sentence case
  readonly value: string; // «23» · «Фейд» · «1 март»
  readonly detail: string | null; // «септември 2026 г.» · «23 пъти» · «2025 г.»
  readonly icon: UiIconName;
  readonly src: string | null;
  readonly isNew: boolean;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface CollectionVm {
  readonly ring: GaugeVm;
  readonly figure: string;
  readonly line: string;
  readonly levels: string;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface MedalSectionVm {
  readonly categoryId: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly count: string;
  readonly tiles: readonly AchievementTileVm[]; // ALL the category's families, in shelf order
  readonly folded: number | null; // the total, when more than 9 families: two rows show
}
export interface MedalsPageVm {
  readonly records: readonly RecordCardVm[];
  readonly chips: readonly AchievementChipVm[];
  readonly close: readonly AchievementTileVm[]; // ≤ 3
  readonly season: SeasonCardVm | null;
  readonly events: readonly EventCardVm[];
  readonly collection: CollectionVm;
  readonly sections: readonly MedalSectionVm[];
  readonly archive: ArchiveRowVm | null;
  readonly emptyHint: string | null;
}
export interface RecordRowVm {
  readonly id: string; // §2.3.4's nine ids
  readonly theme: 'rhythm' | 'visits' | 'favourites';
  readonly title: string;
  readonly figure: string;
  readonly line: string | null;
  readonly icon: UiIconName | null;
  readonly src: string | null;
  readonly faceName: string | null;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface RecordsPageVm {
  readonly subline: string;
  readonly featured: (RecordCardVm & { readonly sentence: string }) | null;
  readonly groups: readonly {
    readonly theme: RecordRowVm['theme'];
    readonly title: string;
    readonly rows: readonly RecordRowVm[];
  }[];
  readonly years:
    | readonly {
        readonly year: string;
        readonly count: string;
        readonly gauge: GaugeVm;
        readonly record: boolean;
        readonly accessibleName: string;
      }[]
    | null;
}
export interface DetailPictureVm {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly caption: string | null; // «Фейд» · «28,00 €»
  readonly src: string | null;
  readonly more: number | null; // «+N» on the fourth
}
export interface DetailHowVm {
  readonly sentence: string;
  readonly pictures: readonly DetailPictureVm[];
  readonly ratio: '16 / 9' | '4 / 3';
  readonly columns: 1 | 2;
  readonly faces: readonly {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly src: string | null;
  }[];
  readonly who: string; // «При всеки бръснар · във всеки салон»
  readonly excluded: string | null;
  readonly steps: readonly string[]; // reserved, [] in this round (DV-B)
}
export interface DetailLevelVm {
  readonly level: number;
  readonly medal: MedalVm; // `complete` when passed, else `notStarted`
  readonly title: string;
  readonly reward: string | null;
  readonly earnedOn: string | null;
  readonly tone: 'passed' | 'next' | 'later';
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface DetailActionVm {
  readonly serviceId: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
}
export interface MedalDetailVm {
  readonly seriesId: string;
  readonly categoryId: string | null;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly medal: MedalVm; // extraLarge
  readonly stateLine: readonly LinePart[];
  readonly progress: {
    readonly value: string;
    readonly unit: string | null;
    readonly what: string;
    readonly remaining: string | null;
    readonly thisVisit: string | null;
  };
  readonly how: DetailHowVm | null; // null for a retired family
  readonly ladder: readonly DetailLevelVm[];
  readonly visits: readonly VisitRowVm[]; // ≤ 5
  readonly related: DetailRelatedVm | null; // ≤ 3 tiles
  readonly action: DetailActionVm | null;
}

// ── «Посещения» ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface VisitsPageVm {
  readonly status: SliceStatus;
  readonly line: readonly LinePart[]; // «23 посещения · през 4 седмици»
  readonly lineName: string;
  readonly facts: readonly LinePart[] | null; // the second line
  readonly cells: readonly HistoryCellVm[]; // kept for DV-D's way back
  readonly filter: VisitFilter;
  readonly offeredFilters: readonly VisitFilter[];
  readonly sections: readonly VisitSectionVm[]; // grain 'month'
  readonly paging: HistoryPagingVm | null;
}
```

`libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/visit-row.vm.ts` (CHANGED, additive): `VisitTrailingFact` and `VisitRowVm.trailingFact` (§3.11). Nothing is removed before W4. NEW `visit-card/visit-card.vm.ts` (§3.10).

**`ClientCardStore`** (CHANGED, additive; S1, W2b). It injects `CLIENT_VOUCHERS_READER` and `LOYALTY_ACTIVITY_READER` (both optional, as every port). NEW public members, each composed from the presenters of §7.3:

```ts
readonly hero: Signal<ClientHeroVm>;
readonly overview: Signal<ClientOverviewVm>;
readonly wallet: Signal<Slice<WalletVm> | null>;
readonly ledger: Signal<LedgerPageVm>;
readonly medalsChip: Signal<string>;                       // 'all' at open
readonly records: Signal<Slice<RecordsPageVm> | null>;
readonly visits: Signal<VisitsPageVm>;
readonly rhythmRule: Signal<StreakRuleSummary | null>;     // the rule in force, for the medal detail's rhythm family
readonly loadedVisits: Signal<readonly Appointment[]>;     // the history's loaded pages, for `countsToward` and «По години»
readonly visitRowsInput: Signal<HistoryRowsInput | null>;  // what `visitsRows` takes: the medal detail words its counted visits with it
readonly historyComplete: Signal<boolean>;                 // no cursor left
setLedgerFilter(kind: ActivityKind): void;
loadMoreLedger(): void;
setMedalsChip(id: string): void;
detailAction(seriesId: string): DetailActionVm | null;     // the dock's verb (§5.2)
```

`achievementsData`, `achievementsContext`, `words`, `subject`, `online`, `displayName`, `open`, `loadMoreHistory`, `setHistoryFilter`, `photoById` are read by the new pages as they are. `retry` takes the two new regions; `barTitle` names the two new page kinds. The built `head`, `glance`, `loyalty`, `history` stay until W4 (the editor and the built bodies read them until their own wave); a computed signal nobody reads costs nothing.

### 4.10 What is live and what is seeded

| Thing                                                                                                                                    | The read / write                                                                   | The data today                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The head, the visits, the photos, the quick actions, the reward tickets                                                                  | live (as built)                                                                    | live                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| The birthday                                                                                                                             | live (`users.birthDate`)                                                           | live; the GIFT is a coupon grant: live data, written today only by the seed and by hand                                                                                                                                                      |
| Gift vouchers by holder                                                                                                                  | live read (NEW port)                                                               | **seeded**: issuing has no caller, so production holds no voucher with a holder until issuing exists. The seed gives Георги one (25,00 € of 50,00 €)                                                                                         |
| Points, tier, stamp cards, medals, records, the journal                                                                                  | live reads of server-written projections                                           | **seeded**: `tools/seed/loyalty.mjs` projects them from the seeded book; `apps/functions` holds no loyalty engine (P3). The seed now also writes `tier`, `reward`, `redemption` and `achievement` journal rows, so the feed shows every kind |
| The rhythm RULE                                                                                                                          | live: the head document, its versions, the callable, the audit row, the admin page | the first version is seeded; every later one is published for real                                                                                                                                                                           |
| The rhythm PROJECTION (`StreakState`)                                                                                                    | live read                                                                          | **seeded**, by the domain's own `projectStreak` through `tools/seed/load-domain.mjs`: publish a new interval, re-run `pnpm run seed:dev`, and Георги's and Мартин's cards move. Real clients have no rhythm until the engine ships           |
| The admin preview                                                                                                                        | computed in the browser from `clientStats`                                         | live data, an ESTIMATE                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Enforcement of the rule on real accounts (the daily job, grandfathering, the recompute, closed-day excuses, the client's pause, notices) | —                                                                                  | **later** (the engine)                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
