## 5. Visit-sheet wiring

`libs/features/staff/dashboard/src/lib/visit-editor/staff-visit-editor.{ts,html}` and `staff-dashboard.html` (X1, W3). The stack's rules (first spec §2.2–§2.3) are unchanged: `push`, `replace`, `pop`, `returnTo`, the depth cap 4, ✓ = `popToLadder`. Between the W2 and the W3 gates the app builds and every built binding works (no input or output of a body was removed); what W3 adds is the two new page kinds, the five new outputs and the dock's verb.

### 5.1 Page kinds

`EditorPageKind` (`staff-visit-editor.ts:775`) gains **`'clientLedger'`** and **`'clientRecords'`** (NEW). `achievementCategory` stays for the scopes `season`, `archive` and `day`; a `category:` scope key is no longer pushed (a category is a chip).

| kind                        | reached from (origin test id)                                                                                | depth    | bar title                       | ‹ goes to       | focus on arrive                                               | focus on return                   |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| `client`                    | the ladder row                                                                                               | 1        | the person's name (on collapse) | the ladder      | the container                                                 | the origin inside the card        |
| `clientLoyalty`             | `client-loyalty-all`, `client-loyalty-points`                                                                | 2        | «Лоялност», **from rest**       | `client`        | the container; with an anchor, the anchored section's heading | the card that pushed              |
| `clientLedger` (NEW)        | `rewards-action-history`, `rewards-ledger-all`                                                               | 3        | «Движение»                      | `clientLoyalty` | the container                                                 | n/a (leaf)                        |
| `clientAchievements`        | `client-achievements-all`; the detail's «Всички» (at depth 4 it replaces the top page, the stack's own rule) | 2        | «Постижения»                    | `client`        | the container; opened on a chip, the chip band                | the tile, card or row that pushed |
| `clientRecords` (NEW)       | `client-records-line` (depth 2), `achievements-records-all`, `achievements-record-{kind}` (depth 3)          | 2–3      | «Рекорди»                       | its `returnTo`  | the container                                                 | the control that pushed           |
| `achievementCategory`       | the season card, an event card, the archive row                                                              | 3–4      | the scope's title               | its `returnTo`  | the container                                                 | the tile that pushed              |
| `achievementDetail`         | a medal: the glance (depth 2), a grid (3–4)                                                                  | 2–4      | the family's title              | its `returnTo`  | the container                                                 | n/a; a related medal REPLACES it  |
| `clientHistory`             | `client-visits-all`                                                                                          | 2        | «Посещения»                     | `client`        | the container                                                 | n/a                               |
| `photo`, `discount`, `scan` | as built                                                                                                     | as built | as built                        | as built        | as built                                                      | as built                          |

### 5.2 Outputs the editor binds, and the dock

| Body                                                            | Output                           | The editor does                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `lib-client-card`                                               | `openRecords` (NEW)              | `push('clientRecords', client.id, originOf('client-records-line'))`                                                                                            |
| `lib-client-card`                                               | `openLoyalty(anchor)`            | as built; the anchor may now be `'wallet'` or `'status'`                                                                                                       |
| `lib-client-loyalty`                                            | `openLedger` (NEW)               | `push('clientLedger', client.id, originOf('rewards-action-history'))`                                                                                          |
| `lib-client-loyalty`                                            | `openCode` (NEW)                 | `resetDiscountEntry()`, then `push('discount', null, originOf('rewards-action-code'))`; the focus arrives on the container (the house rule), owner's card only |
| `lib-client-loyalty`                                            | `openReward`, `removeReward`     | as built                                                                                                                                                       |
| `lib-client-achievements`                                       | `openRecords` (NEW)              | `push('clientRecords', …)`                                                                                                                                     |
| `lib-achievement-detail`                                        | `openCategory(categoryId)` (NEW) | `cardStore.setMedalsChip(categoryId)`, then `push('clientAchievements', …)` (a replace at depth 4); the page arrives with its chip band under the bar          |
| `lib-client-ledger`, `lib-client-records`, `lib-client-history` | none                             | —                                                                                                                                                              |

**The dock's verb on a medal's page** (DV-C). The editor's dock (`staff-visit-editor.html:2611-2906`) already branches on the page kind; it gains a branch for `achievementDetail`, before the closing `@else if (dockPage !== null)` that draws ✓ alone:

```html
} @else if (dockPage === 'achievementDetail') { @if (detailAction(); as action)
{
<button
  type="button"
  uiButton
  uiButtonStyle="bordered"
  uiControlSize="large"
  [attr.aria-label]="action.accessibleName"
  data-testid="staff-visit-detail-add"
  (click)="addCountedService(action.serviceId)"
>
  <ui-icon uiName="action.add" /> {{ action.label }}
</button>
}
<!-- …then the ✓, exactly as the generic branch draws it -->
```

`detailAction = computed(() => cardStore()?.detailAction(currentPage()?.subjectId ?? '') ?? null)`. `addCountedService(serviceId)` follows the `repeatLast` precedent (`staff-visit-editor.ts:6612`): one `DraftLeg` for this person from `uiServices()`, in the visit's chair; it STAYS on the page; the sheet's polite region says «Добавихме фейд към посещението.»; nothing is written, «Запази» appears by the dirty rule; the button gives way (the store's `detailAction` answers `null` once the legs hold the service) and the progress card gains its line «Това посещение се брои…».

### 5.3 The always-titled wallet

`pageHasNoLargeTitle` (`staff-visit-editor.ts:1876`) becomes `kind === 'photo' || kind === 'clientLoyalty'`; the shell already ORs it into `titleAlwaysVisible` (`staff-dashboard.html:1139`). The wallet's `h2` is visually hidden with the page's title id, exactly as the photo page's (`staff-visit-editor.html:931`). A bar without a large title collapses on any scroll (`sheet-header.ts:110-114`); the wallet's body asks for the tight fade (`data-sheet-fade="tight"`, §3.3), so the bar's fade keeps to the bar and the pass passes under a crisp edge. `barTitle` (`:1937`) names `clientLedger` («Движение», `clientCard.ledgerPage.title`) and `clientRecords` («Рекорди», `clientCard.achievements.records`).

### 5.4 The rest

- `confirmPage()` treats the two new kinds as client-stack pages: ✓ = `popToLadder()`.
- `lib-staff-visit-editor` passes `occasion` on each `VisitEditorGrantOption` (`staff-visit-editor.ts:618`) through to `ClientCardContext.grants`.
- The discount page's tickets stop passing `uiAction` and project the prominent pill into `[uiCouponAction]` (§3.4): after it, `uiAction` has no caller.
- The accent leftovers in this pair of files are NOT X1's: A1 moves them in W1 (§1.3).
- `apps/web/src/app/app.config.ts` binds `STREAK_DEFINITION_REPOSITORY`, `CLIENT_VOUCHERS_READER`, `LOYALTY_ACTIVITY_READER`, `CLIENT_STATS_DIRECTORY` with `provideLazyPort`; `app.routes.ts` gains `admin/loyalty` (a redirect) and `admin/loyalty/rhythm` with its two guards; the shell's menu gains the admin's entry (X2).

## 6. Copy keys, icon keys, tokens

### 6.1 i18n keys (`libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/assets/i18n/{bg,en}.json`, nested)

Reuse first. K1 only ADDS in W0; every removal and rename below happens in W4, when the built pages that read the old keys are gone (§7.1 Z4). **Reused as they are** (no change): `clientCard.head.since`, `.firstVisit`, `.guest`, `.pausedUntil`, `.pausedPermanent`; `clientCard.capsules.label`; `clientCard.capsule.noShows.*`; `clientCard.plusCount`; `clientCard.offline`; every `clientCard.actions.*`; every `clientCard.reward.*`; `clientCard.visits.title`, `.allName`, `.waitlist`, `.waitlistName`, `.daypart.*`, `.waitlistMore*`; `clientCard.loyalty.title`, `.all`, `.allName`, `.pointsUnit`, `.enoughFor`, `.toNext`, `.pointsName`, `.stamps`, `.stampsName`, `.rhythm*`; `clientCard.achievements.title`, `.levels.*`, `.allName`, `.cue*`, `.summaryName`, `.events`, `.records`, `.archive`, `.toLevel*`, `.complete`, `.withThisVisit`, `.nextWithThisVisit`, `.season*`, `.familyCount`, `.event*`, `.section*`, `.firstHint`, `.record.*`; `clientCard.new`; `clientCard.frozen`; every `clientCard.failed.*`; `clientCard.loyaltyPage.balanceCaption`, `.balanceName`, `.owesCaption`, `.rhythm`, `.tier`, `.moreToGo`, `.availableFrom`, `.perClient`, `.rewardName.*`, `.stampsFigure`, `.stampsToNext.*`, `.rhythmBest`, `.tier*`, `.catalogName`; every `clientCard.rhythm.*` and `clientCard.ledger.*`; every `clientCard.achievement.*`; every `clientCard.history.*` except the two below; `visits.*`; `appointments.status.*`; `account.upcoming.*`; `staff.visit.useNow`, `.remove`, `.removeClient`, `.photos`, `.photoOpen`; `staff.day.visitCall`.

A head line's FACTS are the sentence's parameters: the presenter cuts `LinePart`s from ONE translated sentence around them, so no sentence is split across keys.

| `clientCard.failed.activity`                     | Движението не се зареди.                                                  | The activity did not load.                                          |

The filter's «Всички» is `clientCard.loyalty.all`; «Статус» is `clientCard.loyaltyPage.tier`; «Използвай» is `staff.visit.useNow`; «Покажи още», «Опитай пак», «Покажи всички» are `visits.more`, `visits.retry`, `visits.filter.clear`; «Това е всичко.» is `clientCard.history.endAll`. The healthy phase has no word (no capsule is drawn for it, §2.2).

**A reward inside a sentence is lower-cased** («Стигат за безплатно оформяне на брада», «Добави фейд»): `inSentence(title, lang)` in `client-card.format.ts` lowers the FIRST letter when it is a Cyrillic capital followed by a lower-case letter, and leaves everything else as written (a Latin brand, an acronym). One helper; no sentence is built by hand.

**New: the rule's sentences** (`rhythmRule.*`, its own root: two features print them).

| `rhythmRule.personal`             | Идва на около {{habit}}, срокът му е {{interval}}.                                | Comes about every {{habit}}; the due date is {{interval}}.                                 |
| `rhythmRule.fallback`             | Срокът е {{interval}}, докато опознаем навика му.                                 | The due date is {{interval}} until we know the habit.                                      |
| `rhythmRule.grace`                | {{days}} гратис след срока.                                                       | {{days}} of grace after the due date.                                                      |
| `rhythmRule.protections`          | До {{max}} защити покриват пропуснат срок; нова на всеки {{n}} посещения в ритъм. | Up to {{max}} protections cover a missed due date; a new one every {{n}} visits in rhythm. |
| `rhythmRule.excuses`              | Час, отменен от салона, не се брои за пропуск.                                    | A visit the shop cancelled is never a miss.                                                |
| `rhythmRule.hold`                 | Записан час до {{days}} след срока пази ритъма.                                   | A booked visit up to {{days}} past due keeps the rhythm.                                   |
| `rhythmRule.repair`               | След прекъсване посещение до {{days}} връща ритъма.                               | After a break, a visit within {{days}} restores the rhythm.                                |
| `rhythmRule.holdCount`            | Пропускът не нулира броя.                                                         | A miss does not reset the count.                                                           |
| `rhythmRule.services`             | Броят се посещения с: {{services}}.                                               | Visits count with: {{services}}.                                                           |
| `rhythmRule.upcoming`             | От {{date}}: {{rule}}                                                             | From {{date}}: {{rule}}                                                                    |
| `rhythmRule.short`                | На всеки {{interval}} · {{grace}} гратис                                          | Every {{interval}} · {{grace}} of grace                                                    |
| `rhythmRule.shortNoGrace`         | На всеки {{interval}}                                                             | Every {{interval}}                                                                         |
| `rhythmRule.off`                  | Изключено. Картата на клиента не показва ритъм. Натрупаното се пази.              | Off. The client card shows no rhythm. What was earned is kept.                             |
| `rhythmRule.weeks.one` / `.other` | {{count}} седмица / {{count}} седмици                                             | {{count}} week / {{count}} weeks                                                           |

Reused keys: `promo.coupon`, `promo.voucher`, `loyalty.points`, `loyalty.tier`, `loyalty.reward`, `loyalty.freeze`, `loyalty.ledger`, `loyalty.stampCard`, `loyalty.category.*`, `loyalty.badge.*`, `location.pin` («Във всеки салон»), `action.add`, `action.filter`, `nav.disclosure`, `nav.back`, `checklist.done`, `connection.offline`, `client.remove`, `client.photos`, `contact.*`, `visit.repeat`, `field.popUp`. The first spec's ledger glyphs (`visit.completed`, `loyalty.badge`, `visit.reopen` in `loyalty.presenter.ts:925-973`) give way to `loyalty.activity.*`. `loyalty.locked` (a padlock) is no longer drawn by the client stack: an out-of-reach reward is a desaturated picture and a bar (§2.2). `client.alert` is NOT added: region C of the glance is reserved, not built. After editing the registry: `pnpm icons:glyphs`, then `pnpm icons:check`.

No other token is added: the pass's and the stage's motif and every wash are `color-mix` of existing tokens; the band's flecks are two existing tokens at full strength (`--sys-color-on-accent`, `--sys-color-media-canvas`); the ring sizes are the disc's rung plus its stroke and gap.

## 7. BUILD PLAN: parallel agents in ONE dirty working tree, the dev stack running

### 7.0 Ground rules (every package)

1. **One tree, uncommitted** (branch `feat/booking-flow`). NEVER `git checkout`, `git stash`, `git reset`, `git clean`. Undo by an inverse edit. Re-read a file before an anchored edit. Never touch `package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `node_modules` (another session's Angular 22.2 bump lives there, LR1).
2. **Shell.** First: `export PATH="$HOME/.nvm/versions/node/v22.22.3/bin:$PATH"`.
3. **The dev stack is RUNNING for the whole round** (the owner's emulators and `nx serve web`). So nobody runs `pnpm run dev`, `nx serve`, an `e2e` target, `firebase emulators:*`, a `test-integration` target of `functions`, or a seed or settle script. ONE exception: S2 runs the non-destructive `pnpm run seed:dev` ONCE, as the last act of its work. A capture is a standalone scratch script against the running server (§7.6), never the nx e2e target (`apps/web/playwright.config.mts` starts its own server).
4. **The tree builds at the end of every wave.** The gate of each wave ends with ONE `pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`, and it must be green before the next wave starts. The owner's dev server therefore serves after every gate.
5. **Additive first (the bridge rule).** W0–W3 ADD: a new file, a new export, a new input with a default, a new member on the store. They never remove or re-type a symbol that a file outside the package still reads. What the round retires is `@deprecated` where it stands and is DELETED in W4, when its last reader is gone. Two consequences: a NEW page's presenter is a NEW file (the built presenter beside it is not rewritten), and a body is re-drawn IN PLACE in ONE package together with its spec.
6. **An agent writes ONLY the files its package owns** (§7.1, §7.4). A change it needs in someone else's file is a line in `scratchpad/v2/contract-deltas.md`, which the lead rules on; it is never an edit.
7. **An agent runs ONLY the spec files it touched.** Angular libs through the LR1 scratch runner, from the repo root: `LIB=<repo-relative lib root> pnpm exec vitest run <the spec files> --config <scratchpad>/gate-a1/lib.analog.config.mjs --configLoader native`. Plain-TS projects (`domain-engagement`, `engagement`, `booking`, `tokens`, `seed-tools`, `functions`): `pnpm nx test <project> -- <the spec file>`. No agent runs `web:build`, a whole suite or a lint of the workspace: the GATES do (§7.2), one at a time, nothing else running.
8. **Templates.** Vitest does not type-check. A package that touched a template or a component runs the LR2 scratch `ngc` for its OWN lib at the end of its work: a scratch tsconfig `scratchpad/v2/gates/ngc/<project>.json` made from the template `scratchpad/gate-a1/tsconfig.visits-ngc.json` (`composite: false`, `references: []`, the lib's `src/**/*.ts` less its specs), then `pnpm exec ngc -p <that file>`. A package is DONE when the output names no file of its own; an error in a file outside the package is reported to the lead, never fixed (inside W1 the data lane is in flight: between R1a and R2b the application lib and the built wallet presenter do not compile, and a lib that imports them sees their errors).
9. **Lint** (LR3): never an `eslint-disable` for `@angular-eslint/template/elements-content`; `uiLabel` names an element. A feature imports no domain lib (§3.0).
10. **Every package ships its specs**, both themes where it draws, and a showcase page for a DS piece.
11. **Build against the contract, not against a neighbour's progress**: the signatures of §7.3, the VMs of §4.9, the skeleton files of W0.

### 7.1 Packages and ownership (disjoint)

Size: every package is one sitting for one agent; the five the review found too large are split (the first issue's five page packages into presenters P and bodies B; its R1, R2 and M1 in two). **Depends on** names contracts, not running code, unless it says "landed". A file may change owner BETWEEN waves, never inside one; the hand-overs are named.

#### W0 · contracts and scaffolds (nothing on screen changes)

| ------ | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ |
| **K1** | Words                   | `libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/assets/i18n/bg.json`, `en.json`; `libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-card.words.ts` + `client-card.words.spec.ts`. ADDS every key of §6.1 and the words' members; removes and renames NOTHING                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | §6.1               |

How: the project rule is to invoke the `nx-generate` skill FIRST; K4 does, and runs the generator with `--dry-run` (flags read from `--help`, never guessed). It APPLIES the generator only if the dry run lists no file outside this row; if it lists `package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, a formatter pass or any other file, K4 scaffolds by copying the seven config files of `libs/features/admin/programs` and editing names. K4 never runs `nx sync` | —                  |

### 7.2 Waves and gates

A gate is run by the lead, once, with nothing else running. Its steps run in this order and the gate stops at the first red. `LR1(<lib root>)` is the WHOLE suite of a lib through the scratch runner; `LR2(<project>)` is the scratch `ngc` of rule 8.

| Wave                     | Runs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Gate                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **W0** contracts         | K1, K2, K3, K4, R0 in parallel                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | **Gate 0.** `node tools/icons/build-material-glyphs.mjs --check`; `pnpm icons:check`; bg and en hold the same key set (a node one-liner over the two files); `pnpm nx test tokens domain-engagement engagement`; `LR1(libs/ui/layout)`; the touched specs of `libs/features/staff/client-card` and `libs/features/shared/visits` (`client-card.words.spec.ts`, `visit-row.vm.spec.ts`); `LR2` for `patterns`, `layout`, `shared-visits`, `staff-client-card`, `admin-loyalty`; `pnpm nx lint patterns layout icons shared-visits staff-client-card admin-loyalty i18n`; **`pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| **W1** foundations       | W1a: A1, D1–D8, V1, V2, and the data lane in its order. W1b: D9. W1c: A2. At most SIX agents at once; D5 starts first (D3 and D6 read its API), R1a first in its lane. The tree may be red INSIDE the wave only along the data lane (R1a → R2b); it is green at the gate | **Gate 1.** `pnpm nx test tokens domain-engagement engagement booking seed-tools functions`; `LR1` for `libs/ui/patterns`, `libs/ui/controls`, `libs/ui/modifiers`, `libs/ui/layout`, `libs/features/shared/visits`, `libs/features/shared/catalog`, `libs/features/staff/dashboard`, `libs/infrastructure/firestore`; the rules integration spec through `scratchpad/gate-a1/firestore-integration.analog.config.mjs` (it talks to the RUNNING emulator under its own project ids, `demo-firestore-*`, never `demo-creativo-test`: it cannot reach the dev data); THE SWEEP: `LR1` for every other Angular lib EXCEPT the three that W2 re-draws (`staff/client-card`, `client/appointments`, `client/account`: their specs pin the old look and are rewritten with their bodies, §7.5); `LR2` for `patterns`, `controls`, `modifiers`, `layout`, `shared-visits`, `shared-catalog`, `staff-dashboard`, `staff-client-card`, `client-appointments`, `client-account`; `pnpm nx lint` for the same projects and `firestore`, `functions`; `pnpm nx build functions` (the running functions emulator reloads and the new callable appears: expected); `pnpm nx run showcase:build --skipSync`; **`pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`**. Then **owner review 1** (§7.6) |
| **W2** the staff feature | W2a: P1–P5, M1a. W2b: S1. W2c: B1–B5, C1, M1b. W2d: K3b                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | **Gate 2.** `LR1` for `libs/features/staff/client-card`, `libs/features/client/appointments`, `libs/features/client/account`, `libs/features/admin/loyalty`, `libs/features/shared/visits`; `LR2` for the same five and `staff-dashboard` (the editor must still compile over the new bodies); `pnpm nx lint staff-client-card client-appointments client-account admin-loyalty shared-visits`; **`pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| **W3** wiring            | X1, X2 in parallel                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | **Gate 3.** `LR1` for `libs/features/staff/dashboard`, `libs/features/shared/shell`; `LR2` for `staff-dashboard`, `shared-shell`; `pnpm nx lint staff-dashboard shared-shell web`; **`pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`**. Then **owner review 2** (§7.6)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| **W4** cleanup           | Z1, Z2, Z3, Z4, E2E in parallel                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | **Gate 4.** everything gates 1–3 ran, once more, now WITH the three consumer libs in the sweep; `LR2` for every project the round touched; `pnpm nx run-many -t lint --skipSync`; E2E's specs, which nobody may RUN here, are type-checked through a scratch tsconfig (`scratchpad/v2/gates/e2e.tsconfig.json`: `noEmit`, `apps/web/e2e/**/*.ts`; the folder has no tsconfig of its own) and linted (`pnpm exec eslint apps/web/e2e`); `pnpm nx run showcase:build --skipSync`; **`pnpm nx run web:build --skipSync`**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

**What is known red, and until when.** From the end of W1 to gate 2 the specs of `staff/client-card`, `client/appointments` and `client/account` that pin the old DS look fail (the tile face, the meter under a medal, the muted card); they are not run at gate 1 and are rewritten by B1–B5 and C1. Nothing else may be red at a gate.

**A spec that a gate finds red and that no package of the wave was asked to change** (a DS change broke a pin in a lib the round does not own, or in a case of `staff-visit-editor.spec.ts` outside A1's named lines) is repaired by ONE fixer the lead starts after the gate's first run: it owns exactly the spec files the gate named, may change an assertion to the DS's new contract, and never touches production code.

**Between the W2 and the W3 gates** the app builds and every built binding works. The wallet's «Код» and «История» emit into nothing and its voucher and activity sections are absent (their ports are bound in W3): nobody reviews the app in that window.

### 7.3 The contracts between packages

| Contract                                                                                                 | Published by | Where                      | Read by                            |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| Keys and the words table                                                                                 | K1           | §6.1; `ClientCardWords`    | every W2 package, V3, M1a, M1b     |
| Icon keys, tokens, the barrels, `nearestScroller`                                                        | K2           | §6.2, §6.3, §3.3           | D*, V2, B*, M1b                    |
| DS public APIs (selectors, inputs, outputs, slots, custom properties)                                    | the spec     | §3.1–§3.8                  | B*, C1, M1b                        |
| The page VMs and the VM fixtures                                                                         | K3           | §4.9; `client-pages.vm.ts` | P1–P5, S1, B1–B5                   |
| `VisitRowVm.trailingFact`, `VisitCardVm`, `lib-visits-browser`'s and `lib-visit-card`'s inputs           | K3           | §3.10, §3.11               | V1, V2, P1, P4, P5, B1, B4, B5, C1 |
| The rhythm's domain, `ActivityEntry`, `Coupon.occasion`                                                  | R0, R1a, R1b | §4.1, §4.2, §4.6, §4.7     | R2a, R2b, R3, R4, S2               |
| The application barrel's re-exports (below), the ports, the documents, the callable's request and result | R0, R2a, R2b | §4.3–§4.7                  | R3, R4, V3, P2, M1a, S1, X2        |
| `rhythmRuleWords`                                                                                        | V3           | §3.11                      | P2, P4, M1a                        |
| The presenters' signatures (below)                                                                       | P1–P5        | this section               | S1, B3, B4                         |
| The store's new members                                                                                  | S1           | §4.9                       | B1–B5, X1                          |
| The bodies' inputs and outputs                                                                           | the spec     | §3.12–§3.16                | X1                                 |
| `AdminRhythm.confirmLeave`, `adminRhythmCanDeactivate`                                                   | M1b, M1a     | §3.17                      | X2                                 |
| The menu's `isAdmin`                                                                                     | X2           | §3.17                      | —                                  |

**The application barrel** (`libs/application/engagement/src/index.ts`). A feature may not import a domain lib, so EVERYTHING a feature names is re-exported BY NAME here. W0 (R0): `type ActivityKind`, `type ActivityEffect`, `type ActivityEntry`. W1 (R2b): `StreakDefinition`, `type StreakDefinitionProps`, `StreakDefinitionError` (the base class: a feature reads `code`, `field`, `value`), `type StreakIntervalProps`, `type StreakQualifierProps`, `StreakDefinitionSchedule`, `type StreakTransition`, `type StreakRuleSummary`, `type StreakRuleChange`, `type StreakRuleDirection`, `STREAK_EXCUSES`, `type StreakExcuse`, `type StreakPeriodFact`, `type StreakFacts`, `projectStreak`, `type StreakDefinitionEntry` (deprecated), `type CouponOccasion`; the ports and their tokens `StreakDefinitionRepository`, `STREAK_DEFINITION_REPOSITORY`, `type StreakDefinitionVersion`, `type PublishStreakDefinition`, `type PublishedStreakDefinition`, `type PublishStreakDefinitionError`, `ClientVouchersReader`, `CLIENT_VOUCHERS_READER`, `LoyaltyActivityReader`, `LOYALTY_ACTIVITY_READER`; the use cases `ObserveStreakDefinitionUseCase`, `PublishStreakDefinitionUseCase`, `PreviewStreakDefinitionUseCase`, `type StreakPreview`, `type PublishOutcome`. `GiftVoucher`, `Coupon`, `StreakState`, `LoyaltyCatalog`, `LoyaltyAccount`, `PersonalRecord` are exported already (`index.ts:21-91`). `libs/application/booking/src/index.ts` (R2b): `ClientStatsDirectory`, `CLIENT_STATS_DIRECTORY`.

**The `export` keywords K3 adds in W0** (a keyword per function; no body, no signature, no name changes), so the new presenter files import the built helpers instead of copying them: in `achievements.presenter.ts` `localeOf`, `progressOf`, `earnedAtOf`, `latestEarned`, `isOneOff`, `isSingleLevel`, `inEndedSeason`, `landsOn`, `familyTile`, `archiveRowOf`, `stateLineOf`, `awardShortOf`, `awardSentenceOf`, `relatedOf` (already exported and used as they are: `medalVm`, `shelfOrdered`, `completesWithThisVisit`, `newestFaceLevel`, `seasonCardVm`, `eventCardVm`, `scopePageVm`, `scopeTitleOf`, `BADGE_EMBLEM_ICONS`, `BADGE_CATEGORY_ICONS`); in `loyalty.presenter.ts` `groupedGrants`, `ticketOf`, `rhythmLabel`, `rhythmStateLine`, `pointsText`, `offered`, `redemptionsOf`, `sliceMap` (already exported: `readyRewards`, `glanceReward`, `LoyaltyFlags`); in `client-card.presenter.ts` `phoneOf`, `sameLegs`, `cueOrder` (already exported: `pauseOf`, `isBirthdayToday`, `displayNameOf`, `quickActionsVm`, `lastWorkedVisit`, `clientPhotoVm`, `photoRailVm`, `isInChairToday`, `capsulesRun`). `history.presenter.ts` exports everything it has. These are FROZEN from gate 0: a change to one of them goes through the lead.

**Presenter signatures S1 composes** (each pure; NEW files; `Words` = `ClientCardWords`):

```ts
// glance.presenter.ts (P1)
export interface HeroInput extends HeadInput {
  // HeadInput as built (client-card.presenter.ts:130)
  readonly tier: {
    readonly account: LoyaltyAccount;
    readonly catalog: LoyaltyCatalog;
  } | null; // null: `loyalty.tiers` off
}
export function clientHeroVm(input: HeroInput): ClientHeroVm;
export function overviewCapsulesVm(
  input: Omit<CapsulesInput, 'tier'>,
): ClientCapsulesVm; // no tier, no birthday
export interface BirthdayInput {
  readonly user: User | null;
  readonly today: string;
  /** The owner's usable grants, the visit's bill; empty for a party member, offline, a gone visit. */
  readonly grants: readonly ClientCardGrant[];
  readonly grantsOnBill: readonly ClientCardGrant[];
  readonly words: Words;
}
export function birthdayVm(input: BirthdayInput): BirthdayVm | null;
export function visitCardsVm(input: VisitsModuleInput): VisitCardsVm; // VisitsModuleInput as built (:388)
export interface LoyaltyGlanceInput extends LoyaltyTilesInput {
  // as built (:565)
  readonly vouchers: readonly GiftVoucher[] | null; // null: not read
  readonly ruleInForce: StreakDefinition | null;
}
export function loyaltyGlanceVm(
  input: LoyaltyGlanceInput,
): LoyaltyGlanceVm | null;
export function medalsGlanceVm(
  data: AchievementsData,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
): MedalsGlanceVm | null;

// wallet.presenter.ts (P2)
export interface WalletInput extends Omit<LoyaltyPageInput, 'ledger'> {
  // LoyaltyPageInput as built (loyalty.presenter.ts:1064)
  readonly holder: string | null;
  readonly sinceMs: number | null;
  readonly vouchers: Slice<readonly GiftVoucher[]> | null;
  readonly activity: Slice<readonly ActivityEntry[]> | null;
  readonly ruleInForce: StreakDefinition | null;
  readonly ruleUpcoming: StreakDefinition | null;
  readonly online: boolean;
  readonly isOwner: boolean;
  readonly visitGone: boolean;
  readonly covers: ReadonlyMap<string, string>; // serviceId → cover
}
export function passVm(input: WalletInput): PassVm;
export function voucherVms(
  vouchers: readonly GiftVoucher[],
  today: string,
  words: Words,
): readonly VoucherVm[];
export function walletHoldings(input: WalletInput): readonly WalletHoldingVm[];
export function walletActions(input: WalletInput): readonly WalletActionVm[];
export function walletRhythmVm(input: WalletInput): WalletRhythmVm | null;
export function walletTierVm(input: WalletInput): WalletTierVm | null;
export function rewardTiles(input: WalletInput): readonly RewardTileVm[];
export function walletVm(input: WalletInput): WalletVm;

// ledger.presenter.ts (P2)
export interface ActivityEvent {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly at: Date;
  readonly entries: readonly ActivityEntry[];
  readonly delta: number | null;
  readonly balanceAfter: number | null;
}
export function foldByEvent(
  entries: readonly ActivityEntry[],
  whole: boolean,
): readonly ActivityEvent[];
export interface ActivityInput {
  readonly visitTitles: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  readonly today: string;
  readonly wholeMonths: ReadonlySet<string>;
  readonly words: Words;
}
export function activityGroups(
  events: readonly ActivityEvent[],
  input: ActivityInput,
): readonly ActivityGroupVm[];
export interface LedgerPageInput extends ActivityInput {
  readonly status: SliceStatus;
  readonly entries: readonly ActivityEntry[];
  readonly next: number | null;
  readonly loadingMore: boolean;
  readonly failedMore: boolean;
  readonly filter: ActivityKind;
  readonly holder: string;
  readonly balance: number;
  readonly online: boolean;
}
export function ledgerPageVm(input: LedgerPageInput): LedgerPageVm;

// medals.presenter.ts (P3)
export function medalRingVm(
  series: BadgeSeries,
  data: AchievementsData,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
  size: UiMedalControlSize,
): MedalVm; // `medalVm` + the fourth state
export function recordCardVm(
  record: PersonalRecord,
  data: AchievementsData,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
): RecordCardVm | null;
export function medalsPageVm(
  data: AchievementsData,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
  chip: string,
): MedalsPageVm;
// records.presenter.ts (P3)
export interface RecordsPageInput {
  readonly data: AchievementsData;
  readonly ctx: AchievementsContext;
  readonly stats: ClientStatsRecord | null;
  readonly ruleInForce: StreakDefinition | null;
  readonly visits: readonly Appointment[];
  readonly historyComplete: boolean;
}
export function recordsPageVm(input: RecordsPageInput): RecordsPageVm;

// medal-detail.presenter.ts (P4)
export function countsToward(
  series: BadgeSeries,
  visit: Appointment,
  subjectUserId: string,
): boolean;
export function detailActionOf(
  series: BadgeSeries,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
  gate: {
    readonly online: boolean;

  },

  seriesId: string,
  data: AchievementsData,
  ctx: AchievementsContext,
  extra: {
    readonly visits: readonly Appointment[];
    readonly subjectUserId: string;
    readonly rule: RhythmRuleWords | null;

  },

// visits-page.presenter.ts (P5)
export function visitsRows(input: HistoryRowsInput): readonly VisitRowVm[]; // HistoryRowsInput as built
export function visitsSections(
  rows: readonly VisitRowVm[],
  filter: VisitFilter,
  words: Words,
): readonly VisitSectionVm[]; // grain 'month'
export interface VisitsPageInput {
  readonly status: SliceStatus;
  readonly rows: HistoryRowsInput;
  readonly filter: VisitFilter;
  readonly stats: ClientStatsRecord | null;
  readonly spend: HistorySpendVm | null;
  readonly paging: HistoryPagingVm | null;
}
export function visitsPageVm(input: VisitsPageInput): VisitsPageVm;
```

`B3` and `B4` call `medalsPageVm` and `medalDetailVm` themselves, over `store.achievementsData()` and `store.achievementsContext()` (the built pattern of those two bodies); everything else reaches a body through a store signal.

### 7.6 The owner's two reviews, and how a capture is made

The round exists because a spec-compliant build was rejected on sight. The owner therefore sees REAL pixels twice, and the first time before a single page is built.

**How.** A capture is a standalone Playwright script in the scratchpad (`scratchpad/v2/gates/shots-*.mjs`, the pattern of `v2/account/shot.mjs`: `node_modules` symlinked beside it, `waitUntil: 'commit'`, sign-in through `window.__e2eSignIn` with a token minted for `dev-staff-ivan`). It READS the running dev server; it writes nothing to the emulators. The showcase is captured from its BUILD (`dist/apps/showcase/browser` behind a static file server), so no second `nx serve` is started. The script also injects `axe-core` and runs the WCAG 2.2 AA tags in both themes, and counts the orange slabs of §1.5 (8).

**Review 1, after gate 1** (the DS is built, no page is): the showcase's composition page and the control pages, 390, light then dark. The owner is shown, and asked, in this order:

1. **Q1** the calendars and the schedule's today and now marks: the week strip as it is, beside the designer's pick.
2. **Q2** the discs: the neutral row (R2-B) over the accent row (his "before").
3. The ring: a medal at each size and the standalone ring, beside `/account`'s ring.
4. The hero with 35 of air, beside the rejected head (`wave-d/r7/shots/martin-light-390-002-glance-rest.png`).
5. The birthday card and the pass.
6. **Q6** whether a staff history row should open its visit (it decides one input of B5).

His answers are written to `scratchpad/v2/owner-answers.md`. W2 starts on the defaults; an answer that differs changes the ONE place its row of §8.1 names, by the package that owns that file.

**Review 2, after gate 3** (the app is wired): at 390 light first, the glance at rest (Георги; Мартин on his birthday), the wallet at rest, «Постижения» at rest, a medal's detail, «Рекорди», «Посещения», «Движение», `/admin/loyalty/rhythm` and one of its sheets, `/account`, `/account/appointments`, the client's visit sheet. Then dark, then 330 and 1024. Beside each capture, the matching `wave-d/r7/shots/` capture: a region that still reads as the old one is a defect. «Посещения» is also set beside `v2/account/appointments.png`: the same screen at rest, or a defect.

**Checks of review 2**, from the script: hover and press bounds on every trailing action at 390 and 1024 (F4: the capsule ends on the column's edge); no horizontal overflow at 330 on any page; at most one orange slab per page; at no scroll position of the glance and of the medal detail is a face, a plate or a stage at more than 10 % opacity while the compact title is at more than 10 %; axe clean in both themes, no waiver.

### 7.7 After the round (NOT in this workflow: it needs the dev stack down)

1. With the dev stack stopped, the lead runs the e2e target once (it starts its own emulators and seeds them): E2E's specs, axe in both themes on every page of the stack and on `/account`, `/account/appointments`, `/admin/loyalty/rhythm`.
2. The screenshot baselines are regenerated ONLY after the owner has accepted review 2. **Decided 2026-10-26 — owner ruling OR-56 (`docs/architecture/decisions/2026-10-26-owner-rulings.md`):** the six baselines pass E regenerated (`account-dashboard-populated-*`, `appointments-list-populated-*`, `appointments-calendar-*`, light and dark) are accepted — they record this revision, the accent law and the date-picker style faithfully. Two defects they show are fixed, each regenerating its own baselines after both images are viewed: an upcoming row's tile names the month (and the year outside the current one, R59 through `yearIfElsewhere`) with the weekday on the fact line — «Иван Колев · сб, 10:00» / "Ivan Kolev · Sat, 10:00" — and a filter row of a single chip is not shown (a system answers "show the chip row" only for 2 or more chips). The mixed voice they also show goes with the «ти» pass (OR-51).
3. `pnpm run verify:bundle` reads `browser-stats.json` under the 22.2 bump (the known rename): the run names the file it found.
4. Before production: deploy the rules and the indexes; `publishStreakDefinition` ships with the functions.

### 8.2 What stays later-phase (listed, never drawn as dead UI)

**From Fresha** (fresha-coverage §6): the staff alert pinned under the head, client notes and tags (P1: the slot C is reserved) · the pause composer (P2) · tier perks (once booking honours one) · staff verbs on the wallet: exchange points for a reward in the chair (the FIRST to build next, Q14), add or deduct points, change the tier, give a reward by hand (P4–P5) · the client's own wallet in the app (P5: the pass, the holdings and the activity list are built as presentational pieces so it can reuse them) · referrals and "referred by" · the client's source · marketing consent, then birthday, win-back and rebook messages · loyalty notifications · reviews · prepaid packages, memberships, account credit, deposits, a saved card, an unpaid balance (all behind payments: each would be one more ticket in «В портфейла») · products (behind retail) · the directory, segments, merge, export, delete (P5) · an Apple or Google Wallet pass · a preferred language · per-voucher detail (code, original value, its own movements) and ISSUING vouchers.

**Rejected for a barbershop** (unchanged): allergies and patch tests (health data), consultation forms and document files, gender, pronouns, addresses, emergency contacts, no-show fees, a Sales tab, an editable balance.

**From this design**: a link from the wallet's rhythm card to the admin's rule page (it needs the editor's discard guard to carry a destination) · a DS pinned chip band (Q15) · numbered steps on a medal whose requirement has distinct actions, and with them a vertical `steps` gauge (DV-B) · record rows that lead into a filtered history (needs a history filter by service and by year) · «Всички 23 ›» on the detail's visits (the same filter) · a staff history row that opens its visit (Q6) · the calendar view inside the staff history · search on «Постижения» (it joins at ≥ 60 families) · a reward tile's picture for a reward that is not one service · the rhythm's engine: the daily job, real per-client projection, grandfathering's enforcement, the recompute, the exact hashed preview, closed-day excuses, the client's own pause, the notice period for a stricter rule.

## 9. Critique resolution

Two reviews of the first issue of this file: **T** = `v2/critique-taste.md` (the owner's lens), **F** = `v2/critique-feasibility.md` (feasibility, house rules, the build plan). Every finding's evidence was opened before it was answered (the code lines in the working tree, the prototypes `v2/ds-defects/{hero,discs}/*.png`, the liked and the rejected captures, and Mobbin for the two patterns that changed most: a loyalty pass and a badge's detail). **Accepted** = done as asked. **Accepted, other fix** = the defect is real, the remedy differs. **Partly** = one part done, one part refused, the reason written. No finding is rejected whole.

| T22 | minor    | Q1 is asked last                                                                                                                                              | **Accepted**                                              | Q1 is the FIRST thing of review 1 (§7.6, §8.1), and it now names the schedule's three marks too (F7)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| T23 | minor    | Copy                                                                                                                                                          | **Accepted** (all ten)                                    | «идва през 4 седмици»; «371 точки» on the pass; a reward lower-cased inside a sentence (`inSentence`, §6.1); «23 пъти» and sentence-case labels; «Почти спечелени»; «Броени посещения»; «Добави фейд»; «Подарък от салона»; «Портфейлът е празен.»; «Първо посещение: 6.03.2025 г.»                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### 9.2 Feasibility, house rules, build plan

| #   | Severity | Finding                                                                                                    | Verdict                           | What changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| F1  | blocker  | The W2 gate type-checks a lib whose store is rewritten in W3; the stub store lowers the specs' fidelity    | **Accepted, other fix**           | The plan is re-cut so the tree builds at the end of EVERY wave (§7.0 rules 4–5). Nothing is rewritten in place: the new pages get new presenter files and new store members BESIDE the built ones (§4.9). Inside W2 the order is presenters → the store (S1, alone) → the bodies, so a body is written over the REAL store and the rig stays on it; there is no stub store |
| F2  | blocker  | "The two VM files compile alone" cannot pass in W0: `ActivityKind` and the fourth medal state arrive in W1 | **Accepted**                      | W0 gains **R0** (the three activity types and their re-exports) and K2 widens the four DS unions (§7.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| F3  | blocker  | The accent guard lands in W1 and is red until W4; guard 1 can never pass as worded                         | **Accepted**                      | The guard strips comments and skips the theme files' declarations; the staff stack's five lines are A1's in W1; the token is deleted at the END of W1 (A2); guards 5 and 7 are recorded allow-lists (§1.5)                                                                                                                                                                 |
| F4  | major    | The plate and the detail's category are on no exit animation: they ghost through the bar's row             | **Accepted**                      | The plate is projected INSIDE the media; the category left the head; the media's timeline is inset by the chrome less the rise; the spec has a case per node (§3.3, §7.5)                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| F5  | major    | `uiForegroundStyle="primary"` writes nothing: the facts stay grey                                          | **Accepted**                      | The rule and the explicit stamp (§1.3, §3.6); the directive and its spec are A1's. Checked: the tree's one bound `'primary'` is in a template W2 re-draws                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| F6  | major    | The pinned chip band cannot be built from the text                                                         | **Accepted, other fix**           | Nothing is pinned (DV-A): no cover, no `modal-sheet.css` clearance, no copy of a DS recipe. The band takes the sheet's own `scroll-margin` (§3.9)                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| F7  | major    | Three small white figures on the solid accent in the staff schedule, in no package                         | **Accepted** (deferred, recorded) | They are deferred WITH the calendars, named in §1.4, in the guard's allow-list and in Q1 (DV-I)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| F8  | major    | Two bound `'promo'` values, two showcase lines and two undecidable guards                                  | **Accepted**                      | The two rows and their spec pins are A1's; so are the two showcase files; guard 6 reads bound expressions; guard 5 is a glyph-suffix rule plus a recorded table that opens with the eight rules named (§1.3, §1.5)                                                                                                                                                         |
| F9  | major    | A `routerLink` inside the sheet drops the visit's draft                                                    | **Accepted, other fix**           | The link is REMOVED from the wallet (§2.2.2): the admin reaches the rule from the menu. The admin page's own guard is the contract `adminRhythmCanDeactivate` (M1a) ↔ the route (X2) (§3.17, §7.3)                                                                                                                                                                         |
| F10 | major    | Files the design changes that nobody owns                                                                  | **Accepted**                      | `modal-sheet.spec.ts` and `sheet-header.spec.ts` no longer change (the header's selector is left alone, §3.3); `ui-metric` gets its rung and an owner (D8); the header and the identity service are X2's; `visit-row.vm.spec.ts` is K3's; the two `testing.ts` are R2b's; every `tsconfig*.json` is K4's (§7.1, §7.4)                                                      |
| F11 | major    | One spec of the glance reads four other pages' stylesheets                                                 | **Accepted**                      | The case keeps its own page; each page's spec takes its own line (§7.5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| F12 | major    | The "verbatim move" of the detail presenter drags ten private helpers                                      | **Accepted, other fix**           | Nothing is moved. K3 adds `export` keywords to the helpers the new files import, listed by name and frozen from gate 0 (§7.3)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| F13 | major    | A feature may not import the domain; the re-exports are in no contract; the graph omits an edge            | **Accepted**                      | The re-export list is R0's and R2b's deliverable, by name (§7.3); the graph names `admin/loyalty → application/booking` and `shared/visits` (§3.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| F14 | major    | The generator may write `package.json`                                                                     | **Accepted**                      | K4: the skill first (the project's rule), `--dry-run`, applied only if it lists nothing outside the package, else the seven files copied; K4 owns every reference; the gates build with `--skipSync` (§7.1, §7.2)                                                                                                                                                          |
| F15 | major    | Five packages are not one sitting                                                                          | **Accepted**                      | The five page packages → P1–P5 and B1–B5; R1 → R1a, R1b; R2 → R2a, R2b; M1 → M1a, M1b.
